# Feature Registry — Zonna

**Job:** What's been built + tier assignments. Single source of truth for "does this exist? is it free or paid?"
**Pair:** When a backlog item ships, the `/ship` skill appends it here. Forward work lives in `docs/releases/backlog.md`.
**Rule:** Every feature must be tagged FREE or PAID here before implementation begins. Untagged features are build blockers.

---

## R23 Rebuild — In Progress

The R23 plan generator is being rebuilt against an updated coaching spec (see `docs/architecture/ADR-009-config-driven-generation.md`, ADR-010, `docs/canonical/CoachingPrinciples.md`). The rebuild is config-driven and catalogue-driven; it does not change tier assignments.

| Concern | Tier (unchanged) |
|---|---|
| Plan generation — generic templates | FREE |
| Plan generation — personalised (rule engine + AI enrichment) | PAID at generation; retained-in-free after trial (Option A) |
| 5K / 10K / HM plans | FREE |
| Marathon / 50K / 100K plans | PAID |
| Dynamic reshape (R20) | PAID — ongoing |
| Strava intelligence | PAID — ongoing |
| AI coach notes (new) | PAID — ongoing |
| Session catalogue — full | granted at trial, retained in free for the trial-era plan |
| Session catalogue — ultra-specific rows (50K/100K) | PAID only (`is_free_tier = false`) |

See `docs/canonical/monetisation-strategy.md` for the Option A tier categories.

## Shipped Features

| Feature | Tier | Release | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| GRID-MARATHON-CAPABLE-01 — the cohort grid can finally express a §24-capable marathoner | FREE (infra) | 2026-09-19 | §24 wants a marathon peak long run ≥ 75% of race distance (31.65 km); §52 caps any single run at 60% of its week; so a runner satisfying both needs a week of **at least ~53 km — and `VOLUMES` topped out at 50**. **Every marathon measurement ever taken on this grid was scoped to runners who could not satisfy §24 in the first place**, which is how *"100% of marathon time-goal plans are maintenance-grade, 0% reach the §24 floor"* got reported as a catastrophic engine finding when it was an artefact of the corpus. `VOLUMES` gains 70: grid 31,104 → 41,472 (+33%, still inside the duration budget), and **§24 is now reached by 68 of 5,184 marathon time-goal rows against 0 before**. ⚠️ **Re-baselined with the moves declared, not hidden:** `maintenancePct` +7.8pp (49.4 → 57.2) and never-builds 10.1 → 18.8 (healthy masters) / 8.1 → 15.6 (healthy standard). **Neither is a behaviour change** — they are §106's documented residual becoming visible, and they surfaced `PEAK-VS-DELIVERED-BUILD-01` (below). |
| PLAN-NOTE-LENGTH-01 — the rationale note is capped at two reasons | FREE (copy) | 2026-09-19 | The widened grid reached a cohort tripping three checks at once, stacking the note to **127 words against a 117-word ratchet**. The test's own instruction is *"shorten the copy, do not raise the ratchet"*, and the SLT's PLAN-NOTE-VOICE-01 ruling is consequence, cause, **one** lever. Two changes: at most two reason clauses survive, and the *"biggest week comes earlier"* line — a second detail on the same point as *"holds steady"* — now only earns its 12 words when it is the note's only reason. ⚠️ **The LAST two reasons are kept, not the first**, because the order is already the priority order: the long-run clause is pushed last and is the one Hutchinson called *"the one genuinely SAFETY-relevant line here"*. |
| STEPBACK-STALE-PEAK-01 — §47's step-back is re-measured against the peak that survived | FREE (coaching infra) | 2026-09-19 | `applyPeakLongRunAlternation` sized the step-back as a ratio of the peak long run **it could see**; `applyLongRunProgressionCap` runs afterwards and can trim that peak, so the ratio ended up measured against a number that no longer existed — measured, a **166-minute step-back against a final peak of 206, 80.6% against §47's 80% bound**. ⚠️ **The set of step-back weeks is RETURNED by §47 rather than re-derived.** A naive re-clamp had been tried and reverted because treating every sub-peak week as a step-back breaks §35 and §24 — only §47 knows which weeks it chose (the exception, the deload interaction and the alternation parity all feed it). ⚠️ **§45 runs AGAIN after the re-clamp, and that is not belt-and-braces:** lowering a step-back raises the step the following week must take, and measured it broke §45 (W15 re-clamped to 23 km turned W16's untouched 29 km into a 26% jump). Same ratchet shape as §114's post-pass attempts; §45 only ever reduces, so a second pass converges. ⚠️ **Two guards caught me on the way in.** `INV-PLAN-LONG-IS-LONGEST` threw 16 hard failures because the re-clamp dropped the long run under §9's long-vs-easy ratio (long 21 km vs easy 17), and `sessionDistanceReach.test.ts` caught the first cut using `distance_km ?? 0` — the SESSION-KM-01 antipattern, in a fix written by someone who had read that memory the same morning. Retires the interim tolerance widening (1 → 2 min) that carried *"fix the ordering instead"* in its own comment. **Declared move:** `cohortGrid/NEVER-BUILDS` 812 → 815 (+3 of 2,868, borderline maintenance-grade plans); priority-one beginner-marathon cohort unchanged, `planFitness` gates green, sweep 14,476 plans no new violations. |
| MAINT-LIVENESS-01 — the maintenance block gets probed, and the recorded reason was wrong | FREE (infra) | 2026-09-19 | Eight `INV-MAINT-*` codes sat in the liveness baseline as `corpus` debt — *"the harness never builds this plan SHAPE"* — for months, and **§67 and §75 rested on two of them**. 🔴 **The recorded reason was wrong and it hid the actual cause.** These invariants are not checked by `validatePlan` at all; they are checked by `validateMaintenanceBlock`, and the harness only ever called `validatePlan`. **The harness was calling the wrong validator**, so no corpus, however wide, could ever have woken them. ⚠️ **Fourth liveness-debt finding, and the first where the SAMPLE was not the problem** — the by-shape dedup (2026-09-12), the shape key (2026-09-15) and the round-robin heads (2026-09-17) were all about which inputs got probed. This one was the call. Adds a maintenance corpus (4 cases spreading base volume, cadence, injury and race distance) plus a 7-mutation battery of its own, on the same deliberate-breakage discipline: a quiet block cannot tell a working rule from a dead one. ⚠️ **The mutations had to name the exact field each rule reads** — the first cut missed four by guessing (`NO-RACE-SPECIFIC` reads `session.category`, not the label; `QUALITY-CAP` counts against a per-week cap so one session is not enough). **Liveness 107/121 (88%) → 118/121 (98%); all 8 maintenance invariants proven.** The §67/§75 coverage debt is discharged for real rather than re-described — `principleCoverage.test.ts` caught the stale baseline entries itself, which is COVERAGE-BITE-01 working. |
| PLAN-QUALITY-AUDIT-01 — the fifth question: would we be PROUD to hand this plan over? | FREE (infra) | 2026-09-19 | `npm run audit:plans`. The other four harnesses each answer a different question — sweep (valid?), parity (unchanged?), `cohort:shape` (who gets what?), `measure:fitness` (does it build?) — and **a plan can pass all four and still be one no coach would hand over**: S52's seven consecutive two-run weeks did. Carries its **own beginner-marathon corpus** (6,480 inputs, cwk 0–30, longest 0–12, 3–5 days, 16–29 week runways, ages 28/42/58, injury and fresh-return toggled) because `cohortGrid` derives longest from volume and never varies injury, and `targetedGrid` pins age 40 — neither can express *"never run before, wants to run London"*. Predicates match §52's own exemptions so counts are not inflated against the invariant that governs them. **First reading on priority one: 36.4% refused; of those built, 49.4% short of declared days, 45.2% with a ≥75% single session, 40.5% with ≤2-run weeks, 32.8% with a >30% week-1 leap.** Committed baseline (`planQualityBaseline.json`), the debt-register pattern — a NEW regression fails, existing debt is visible. ✅ **GATED in `npm run verify` the same day** — the baseline makes existing debt visible rather than blocking, so gating costs 18 s and stops the numbers regressing silently. It shipped ungated for about an hour, which this repo's history says is a check that does not run (`measure:fitness` shipped ungated and that was recorded as the mistake). |
| INV-MSG-ROUNDING-01 — a violation message no longer reads as self-contradictory | FREE (infra) | 2026-09-19 | Two invariant messages rounded BOTH sides to integers, so a real breach rendered as *"Got 18 min, expected ≤ 18 min"* — a message asserting the check fired on a value that satisfies it. ⚠️ **Cosmetic in effect, corrosive in practice:** the next person to hit it concludes the invariant is broken and goes looking for a bug that is not there, which is a cost this repo has paid before. One decimal on both sides of the comparison in `INV-PLAN-MAIN-SET-ORDERING` and `INV-PLAN-EFFORT-GOVERNED-DURATION-LOWER-BOUND`. 2,341 tests green. |
| CB-HILL-INJURY-01 — hill strides yield to §21 (§28 Am.2) | FREE (coaching) | 2026-09-19 | 🔴 **A LIVE SAFETY DEFECT, shipped by §28 Am.1 on 2026-09-18 and closed the next day.** `isHillStrideWeek()` keyed on week number and fitness level and **never consulted `injury_history`**, so a beginner with knee history was prescribed *"6×10s hill strides up a moderate gradient"* in weeks 5 and 9 — with `'knee'` in `HILL_RESTRICTING_INJURIES` and §21 barring exactly that. ⚠️ **INVISIBLE BECAUSE THE LABEL LIED.** `INV-PLAN-INJURY-NO-HILLS` classifies hill work by matching the session LABEL; the label said `Easy run — Zone 2` while the coach note said hill strides, so a §21 safety invariant was looking straight at the session and could not see it. **No measurement found this — making a label honest did** (STRIDE-VISIBILITY-01, same commit). The D-17 failure mode catching itself. ⚠️ **The predicate existed THREE times before it existed once** (`ruleEngine` selector, `invariants` §21 check, and a fourth copy about to be written) — that omission IS how it reached production, because the producer never asked the question the checker was asking. Single owner now: `neuromuscular.ts → hasHillRestrictingInjury()`. A barred runner gets §28's flat strides every stride week: a change of modality, not a loss of stimulus. |
| STRIDE-VISIBILITY-01 — the label says the session carries strides | FREE | 2026-09-19 | **SLT ruled BUILD 2026-09-18, built in the 2026-09-19 batch.** §28 places strides on **100% of eligible weeks — 22,558 of 22,558 measured**, mean 10.1 stride runs per plan — and every one was labelled `Easy run — Zone 2`, byte-identical to a plain easy run. Now `Easy run + strides — Zone 2` / `+ hill strides`. Derived at the point of placement from the structural fact that strides were placed; **nothing branches on the string** (D-17). Wood's binding condition held: descriptive, never congratulatory, no chip, no description change. ⚠️ **It exposed two defects on the way in.** (1) CB-HILL-INJURY-01 above. (2) `hasStrideNote()` carried a `label` arm that was DEAD while no label said "strides" and became a hole the instant one did — the enricher can rewrite labels, so a stripped stride note under an intact label would have passed the net that exists because the enricher once stripped notes from 12 of 17 weeks of a live plan (ENRICH-STRIDES-01). Arm removed: the note is the prescription, the label is display. Parity moves on 100% of plans, as the SLT predicted. 7 golden snapshots re-based; `planRowLabel` duplication threshold re-based 0.5 → 0.4 with the reason recorded (the label now carries MORE than the chip, which strengthens the no-chip decision). |
| TAPER-FLOOR-FLAT-01 — the taper reads runner-resolved floors | FREE (coaching) | 2026-09-19 | Three reads of the flat `MIN_SESSION_DISTANCE_KM` in `taperRecalibration.ts` — the last producer site still on it after §113 Am.1 moved everything else to `sessionFloorsFor`. ⚠️ **The item was filed claiming it "cannot bind", then that it binds only at 5K. Measured across 6,738 taper weeks it pins a session at the flat easy floor on 38.8% of them** — the filed severity was wrong in both directions. A plan with no recorded `generator_input` resolves to the configured floors unchanged. 17 taper tests green. |
| XREF-DANGLE-01 — a backlog line that PROMISES an entry must deliver one | FREE (infra) | 2026-09-19 | `da96f3c` removed `LR-ABS-ALLOWANCE-01` as a duplicate on 2026-09-17, repointed **five** files (CoachingPrinciples §94 Am.1, `plan-invariants.md`, `feature-registry.md`, the decision record, `invariants.ts`) and **missed one back-reference in `backlog.md`, the file it was editing**. That line went on reading *"filed as … below"* with nothing below, and survived every audit since — `audit-docs.sh` reported ALL CLEAN throughout, because an unkept forward reference is in none of its five categories. Same "flagged but unfiled" class as the `longest_recent_run_km` gate that sat in a code comment until the founder asked why it was not on the list. ⚠️ **THE NARROWNESS IS THE DESIGN AND IT WAS MEASURED, NOT ASSUMED.** The obvious check — every item id must resolve to a definition — was built first and returned **156 hits**, nearly all historical references to closed work plus capitalised prose (`DROP-OUT`, `NO-OP`, `JSON-LD`); this repo has recorded three times that a check which cries wolf gets switched off, which is the same as having no check. Scanning only sentences that assert the entry is *here* ("filed as X **below**") gave **5 promises, 2 unkept, 0 false positives** — and the guard found the second one (`GRID-COVERAGE-TRAINING-AGE-01`) on its own, which reading the file had not. ⚠️ **Neither unkept promise was abandoned WORK** — both were delivered under a different id (`LR-ABS-CAP-LOWVOL-01` and `GRID-COVERAGE-01` respectively) and never reconciled. What rots is the bookkeeping, which is what makes an open list wrong. Falsified against two mutations: reintroduce an unkept promise → red; strip every promise → the vacuity guard goes red (this repo has shipped a regression comparison that ran on two EMPTY files and reported success). `lib/docs/itemXref.test.ts`, 2 tests, inside `npm run verify`. |
| Peak step-back is a volume step-back (§47 Amendment 2 / PEAK-STEPBACK-VOLUME-01) | FREE (coaching) | 2026-09-16 | Coaching Board (test.test marathon review). §47 eased a peak step-back week's long run and stamped "absorb last week's peak", but the WEEK kept climbing on the volume curve — measured, 6,720 plans (22.5%) delivered a step-back week bigger than the week before it (test case ran 45→50→54 km, a "recovery" week 5 km heavier than the one it recovered from). §90's principle ("a week told to be easier must DELIVER less") applied in peak: `applyPeakStepBackVolume` trims easy volume toward `PEAK_STEPBACK_WEEK_MAX_PCT` (90%) of the preceding week, never the long run (§52/§90) or below the min-session floor. **Scoped non-injury** — injury peak volume is owned by §90/§2's injury reconciliation, which runs later (the property sweep, not cohortGrid, surfaced that interaction). Board ruling **Lever A CORRECT**; the companion long-run-cap question (Lever B) was ruled INSUFFICIENT EVIDENCE and filed as a scoped measurement (LR-ABS-CAP-LOWVOL-01). Three artifacts: §47 Am.2, `PEAK_STEPBACK_WEEK_MAX_PCT`, `INV-PLAN-PEAK-STEPBACK-VOLUME`. test.test now runs 45→41→54; verify green (2040 tests, sweep clean). |
| Session-card figures reconcile to the total (SESSION-RECONCILE-01) | FREE | 2026-09-16 | Founder-reported: a 22 km MP long run showed "2 km warm-up · 9 km main · 2 km cool-down" = 13 against a 22 km header, because the race-pace segment (§25, up to 40% of the run) rendered as a bare "40%" with no km/min and each part rounded independently. Two existing tests *claimed* to guard this and both were blind — `sessionComposer.test.ts` summed only 3 parts (never the segment), `racePaceOverlay.test.ts` summed the segment but in minutes computed locally, and neither asserted on the rendered figure. Fix (display only, ADR-015 territory, **Coaching Board exempt**): `race_pace_segment` gains `duration_mins`+`distance_km` and is stamped/counted (`sessionComposer.ts`); new single-owner `resolveDisplayFigures` (`sessionSteps.ts`) folds the segment into the main-set total and apportions whole-unit distances (`apportionRoundedDistance`, `format.ts`) so warm-up+main+cool-down = the session total, both metrics, both units; the card renders the segment's real km/min instead of "40%" (`SessionSteps.tsx`). Guard: `sessionReconcile.test.ts` composes every shape across a sampled cohort and asserts reconciliation (in `npm run verify`) + `apportionRoundedDistance` unit tests. No engine/plan_json/invariant change; weekly totals already reconciled. Contract: `ui-patterns.md` §21b. |

| On-page SEO — marketing homepage (SEO-01) | FREE | 2026-09-02 | *Not in backlog — scoped mid-flight.* Marketing surface only (`app/page.tsx`); no app surface touched. **Title** keyword-first, brand last (`Running Plans to Stop You Overtraining | Zonna`) — deliberately NOT `BRAND.appStoreSubtitle`, which stays brand-first and still drives og:/twitter: titles where that is correct for social sharing. **Meta description** rewritten to 148 chars (inside Google's ~155 snippet budget); og:/twitter: descriptions untouched. **H1** "Plans…" → "Running plans to stop you overtraining", which required DECOUPLING it from `BRAND.appStoreSubtitle` — the subtitle is capped at 30 chars by Apple and sits at 30/30, so it can never carry the "running" keyword. New constant `BRAND.marketingH1` (38 chars, web only) holds it; both constants document why they must not be merged. **Problem H2** "grey middle" → "grey zone" (en_GB "grey" retained). **JSON-LD `SoftwareApplication`** added (none existed anywhere) — placed on the page, NOT the root layout, which also serves `/dashboard` and `/auth`; there is no marketing-specific layout. Price reads `PRICING.monthly.amount`/`PRICING.currency` and installUrl reads `BRAND.appStore.url` rather than literals, so a price or listing change cannot leave the rich result stale. **No `aggregateRating`** — no ratings exist and inventing one is a Google manual-action risk. Verified against RENDERED HTML (built, served, curled `/`), not source: og:title, twitter:title, og:description and twitter:description all byte-identical to before. Also corrected a live doc/code divergence — CLAUDE.md, `brand.md` and `brand-product-alignment.md` documented the subtitle as the 42-char "Training plans that stop you overtraining.", which is not the stored value and would breach Apple's cap — and a `journey-test.md` assertion the H1 change had broken. **Validated:** live on `zonna.run` and passed Google's Rich Results Test 2026-09-02. No open items. |
| Lopsided-week maintenance trigger — §52 fully closed (§52-LOW-VOLUME-LONG-RUN) | FREE | 2026-09-02 | **The §52 violation class is now zero, including the 35 that pre-dated this session.** §52 names three remedies for a week whose long run exceeds `LONG_RUN_MAX_PCT_OF_WEEKLY` — *"reduce the long run, raise weekly volume, or downgrade to maintenance"* — and the engine took none of them: it built the lopsided week and let the invariant report the runner's plan as defective for a constraint the engine itself had chosen. **Cause:** the long run is race-anchored (§45/§47 floors) while the week is runner-anchored (§2 ramp off current volume); at low volume they diverge until the week is lopsided by construction (5 km/wk → HM gives a 14.5 km long run on a 24 km week = 60.4%). **Fix:** takes §52's third remedy — such a plan classifies `maintenance`, which is already exempt from this cap and already carries an honest runner-facing note (new `lopsidedNote`: the race sets the long run, your current volume sets everything else; the lever is weekly volume, not a longer long run). Reducing the long run instead would collide with §45/§47, so this is the remedy needing no new doctrine. **Measured cost:** maintenance classification 131 → 137 of 315 (+1.9pp, 6 plans) — narrowly scoped. **Sweep baseline `INV-PLAN-LR-MAX-WEEKLY-PCT` 59 → 0.** **Falsification-tested:** disabling the trigger brings all 59 violations straight back. **Two earlier hypotheses were disproven by measurement and abandoned before shipping** — "the week shrinks when quality displaces an easy run" (the week is exactly on target; a bounded easy top-up was built, measured, reverted, +1,479 weekday-cap violations). Recorded so neither is re-run. `npm run verify` exit 0 (unpiped), 760 tests, matrix 14/14, 18,060 plans / 0 violations. |
| Verification reaches the assessed path (SWEEP-COVERAGE-01/02) | FREE (infra) | 2026-09-02 | **The check that lets every other check mean something.** The property sweep set `fitness_level` on all 17,957 of its plans and never set `training_age`, so `assessFitness`, the §79 training-age lift and the whole structural-vs-intensity split were **unreachable by it** — it reported "byte-identical, no new violations" on two defects it structurally could not see. **Sweep** grid widened (`fitnessSets` gains `undefined`; `trainingAgeSets` + `declaredSets` added, spread conditionally so absent means absent) → 18,060 plans reaching the assessed path. **Matrix** had the identical blind spot — all 11 archetypes supplied `fitness_level`. Three added: signals-agree derivation, the §79 returning-runner lift (structural `beginner` + intensity `intermediate`, the founder case), and an upward `user_declared_level` asserting `peak_km_target` equals the *structural* band. New `expect` keys assert what the engine DERIVED (`derived_structural`, `derived_intensity`, `peak_target_is_structural`), so an archetype cannot pass merely by generating a plan. **Falsification-tested, not assumed:** re-breaking §79-PEAKKM → matrix 13/14; disabling the returning lift → 3 metrics fail; routing quality COUNT back through intensity → sweep catches the §53 variety violations. **Also fixed:** the matrix compared every plan's taper against the UNSCALED target, so any low-volume archetype reported a false failure — a 32 km-peak plan tapering 21% was measured against 35% while the engine obeyed the depth-scaled PV2 rule exactly. Target now applies `LOW_VOLUME_TAPER_REDUCTION_FACTOR_PCT` below `LOW_VOLUME_TAPER_THRESHOLD_KM`. Matrix 14/14, `npm run verify` exit 0 (checked unpiped — piping through `tail` returns tail's exit code). |
| User-selectable fitness level binds asymmetrically (§79-PEAKKM) | FREE | 2026-09-02 | **Coaching Board veto on shipped behaviour, then fixed.** §79 stated that a user override "raises the **intensity** allowance only; the structural volume/ramp/long-run caps stay bound to the conservative assessment" — the engine did the opposite. A declared level set `fitness`, which set `peakKm`, which sets the **week-1 volume floor** via `BUILD_VOL_INIT_FLOOR_VS_PEAK` (`Math.max(startKm, peakKm × 35%)`), so a dropdown raised *starting* tonnage above the runner's actual current volume. Measured: 10K on 15 km/wk declaring `experienced` went wk1 13→20, peak 18→35; a `<6mo` novice on 8 km/wk took a marathon peak 42→55, straight through `BEGINNER_WEEK1_VOLUME_CAP_KM` (which caps the declared *start*, not the peak-derived floor). **Fix — two inputs, two axes:** new `user_declared_level` carries the wizard's selection; `fitness_level` keeps its API meaning as the structural declaration (**no contract break** — the property sweep is byte-identical pre/post: 17,957 plans, 0 hard failures, same 35 baselined violations). The declaration binds **asymmetrically** — upward raises intensity only, downward binds both — mirroring §50/HR-MAX-01's evidential asymmetry: a runner declaring *less* is credible about their own caution; declaring *more* claims a tissue tolerance nothing has demonstrated. **Also fixed (found while tracing):** the quality-per-week ceiling now keys off the intensity level, closing two holes — it self-skipped entirely when `fitness_level` was absent (i.e. on the whole wizard-accept path, so it was unenforced for most real users), and `validateReshapedPlan` reconstructing input from meta would have failed legitimately-built elevated-intensity plans on every reshape (throw in test, false `reshape_invalid` in prod). Meta now stamps `fitness_level_declared` + `fitness_intensity_level`-on-difference. **Follow-on same day (§79-INTENSITY-ROUTING, board CORRECT WITH AMENDMENT):** secondary-quality selection and quality-session sizing now read the INTENSITY level, as the primary slot already did — slot 2 filtered rows at the structural level where **0 of 14** quality rows are eligible at `beginner`, so it fell through to an **uncatalogued** session (`catalogue_id=NONE`, the ADR-018 no-rep-structure class); measured `10K-pace intervals [NONE]` → `10K-pace sustained [tempo_continuous]`. VO2max dose likewise: a session *selected* at intermediate was *sized* at `VO2MAX_WORK_MIN_MINS` because `VO2MAX_WORK_TARGET_MINS` has no `beginner` band. Board amendment: do NOT add one — a beginner reaches that table only via a lifted intensity, where `intermediate` is correct. **Scope note (a claim made and withdrawn):** this was first written up as "returning runners are denied their second quality session". It is not — `QUALITY_SESSIONS_PER_WEEK_MAX` is a ceiling, not an entitlement, and `plannedQuality` gives every non-`experienced` runner 1 quality session in build/peak by design. Session COUNT is unchanged (measured 1/2 peak weeks before and after); only catalogue linkage and dose sizing changed. **The wizard passes the level whether accepted or overridden**, closing the honesty seam where an accepted level never reached the engine. Three artifacts: §79 amended + `GENERATION_CONFIG.USER_DECLARED_LEVEL_BINDS_STRUCTURE_DOWNWARD_ONLY` + `INV-PLAN-USER-LEVEL-NO-UPWARD-TONNAGE`. `userDeclaredLevel.test.ts` (8, incl. a guard asserting the invariant *fires* on the old behaviour). Analysis: `docs/investigations/79-peakkm-impact-2026-09-02.md`. |
| Metric toggle both ways + duration-anchored finish-goal long run (§80, Phase 3) | FREE | 2026-08-31 | *Not in backlog — scoped mid-flight off §79.* **Part A:** the distance/duration toggle showed "—" when a duration-primary session (beginner / returning / time-on-feet) carried `duration_mins` but no `distance_km`; `SessionPopupInner` now derives distance from duration and marks it `~` as an estimate — never as an exact prescribed target (Coaching Board amendment). Exact distances still render without the `~`. **Part B:** §79 keyed the default metric to experience, so a finish-goal HM/marathon runner can now default to distance — but the §80 peak long run's prescription *is* time on feet, so it is pinned duration-anchored regardless of preference ("two and a half hours of moving" is a different object from "18 km"; a distance headline would make a walk break look like a failed target). Distance retained as a secondary value. Three artifacts: §80 "Duration-anchored display" subsection + no new numeric (a display-authority rule on the session the existing §80 constants already build, stated explicitly) + `INV-PLAN-DURATION-ANCHORED-KEEPS-MINUTES` keyed on the structural `Session.duration_anchored` flag, not a label. `durationAnchoredLongRun.test.ts`; 6 finish-goal peak long runs (P0/P5/P6) flip to duration-primary in the golden snapshots. **Verified on device 2026-09-02.** |
| User-selectable fitness level with engine recommendation (§79, Phase 2) | FREE | 2026-08-31 | *Not in backlog — scoped mid-flight off Phase 1.* New "Where are you right now?" wizard step after training-age: the engine recommends a level (tagged + pre-selected) and the runner can accept or override, with a "why" line and honest override notes — going harder shows the "we still ramp volume gently" safety line (§79/§10: agency raises intensity, never tonnage). Classifier extracted to `lib/plan/fitnessAssessment.ts` as the single owner shared by engine and wizard (same pattern as `maxHrGuard.ts`) — pure refactor, no behaviour change; adds `recommendFitnessLevel()`. **Correctness detail:** `fitness_level` reaches the engine ONLY on a genuine override; accepting the recommendation passes `undefined` so the Phase-1 assessment stands (a returning runner keeps conservative volume + lifted intensity, and no ordinary runner is pushed into a maintenance label). Draft persistence + restore include the level. **Open board follow-up:** the peakKm-binding nuance (see backlog). **Verified on device 2026-09-02.** |
| Returning runner gets quality + distance, not a beginner plan (§79, Phase 1) | FREE | 2026-08-31 | *Not in backlog — founder-reported defect, emergent.* An experienced runner whose volume was down (post-race) was classified beginner on **current volume alone** → `QUALITY_SESSIONS_PER_WEEK_MAX.beginner = 0` stripped every tempo/threshold/interval/hill session, and sessions went duration-primary. A lifelong runner in a dip was handed a true beginner's 10K plan. Coaching Board CORRECT WITH AMENDMENT: `training_age` becomes a third signal consulted for **intensity only** — a deep training age reading beginner-on-volume is a *returning* runner, so intensity lifts off the beginner floor to intermediate, while structure (volume, peak km, ramp, long-run caps) stays bound to current volume per §2/§29/§371. The metric recommendation now follows experience/intensity rather than raw volume, so a returning runner sees distance while a true beginner (all signals agreeing) still gets duration. **Willy, mandatory:** progressive intensity re-entry — VO2max intervals + hill reps (category `vo2max`) withheld for the opening `RETURNING_RUNNER_INTENSITY_REENTRY_WEEKS` while tissue tolerance rebuilds (the aerobic engine returns ahead of the tendons); tempo/threshold carry quality first. Three artifacts: §79 "Returning runners & user-selectable level" + `GENERATION_CONFIG.RETURNING_RUNNER_INTENSITY_REENTRY_WEEKS = 4` + `INV-PLAN-RETURNING-INTENSITY-REENTRY` (structural detection via `catalogue_id` → category, ADR-018 — not label matching). `returningRunnerIntensity.test.ts`. |
| Asymmetric §50 max-HR guard — device floor rejected (HR-MAX-01) | FREE | 2026-08-31 | Apple Health's observed max HR is the highest rate the watch *happened to record* — a **floor**, not a maximum — and `fetchAppleHealthHRSnapshot` was writing it over the better Tanaka age estimate on connect, running HR zones and every plan target ~15% low. Founder evidence: age 44, observed **159**, true **188**, age estimate **177** — connecting Apple Health made the max *worse*, and §50's symmetric ±15% plausibility guard didn't catch it (159 is only ~10% below 177). **Part 1 (Coaching Board CORRECT WITH AMENDMENT):** §50 made asymmetric — a recorded HR is by construction a lower bound, so *above* the estimate is trusted up to `MAX_HR_PLAUSIBILITY_DEVIATION_PCT` (it must have physically occurred) while *below* it a device-observed or unattributed value is rejected (`MAX_HR_BELOW_ESTIMATE_TOLERANCE_PCT = 0`); only `user_confirmed` provenance is trusted below the estimate. Single owner `lib/plan/maxHrGuard.ts` (`resolveMaxHr`, `tanakaMaxHR`) shared by engine + client so the two can't drift; `INV-PLAN-MAX-HR-NOT-BELOW-ESTIMATE-FLOOR`. **Part 2:** the client computes zones from a §50-guarded `effectiveMaxHR` fed to every display surface (Today / Session / Coach / Orientation / Strava HR targets, `effectiveZone2Ceiling`, aerobic pace) so session targets no longer recompute from the raw floor; the MeScreen HR editor shows guarded zones + a plain-language floor note with a recovery path ("If 159 really is your max, tap Save to confirm it") — **not a silent correction**. Save tags `user_confirmed`; connect paths (ConnectRuns + Settings reconnect) tag `observed`. **Part 3:** `fetchAppleHealthHRSnapshot` scans running-workout HR peaks across the full 90-day window via bounded pagination, replacing `limit: 5000` most-recent raw samples (which on a continuously-logging watch spanned ~1–2 days, depressing the observed max further). Migration `20260831_max_hr_source.sql` adds `user_settings.max_hr_source` (`observed` \| `user_confirmed` \| NULL = unattributed, guarded as a floor, self-healing on next save) — **applied to prod 2026-09-02, verified**. `maxHrGuard.test.ts` (10). **Verified on device 2026-09-02.** |

| **Recalibration 5K time trial** (GEN-FIX-04) | **FREE** | Wave 1b, 2026-08-06 | CoachingPrinciples §78. Deload weeks in base/build convert their midweek easy run into a 5K time trial — same distance and duration, so weekly volume is unchanged. Typed `hard` (Z4-5), **not** `quality`, so it does not count against `QUALITY_SESSIONS_PER_WEEK_MAX` and **beginners on zero-quality plans get it too** — a benchmark is a measurement, not a training stimulus. No pace target by design: the session exists to discover current pace, not rehearse the stale one. `meta.recalibration_weeks` is now derived from the produced plan, so a week with no placeable slot is simply not listed. Enforced by `INV-PLAN-RECALIBRATION-HAS-SESSION`. |
| **Plan-generator regression harness** (GEN-FIX-08) | **FREE** (infra) | Wave 1b, 2026-08-06 | Golden snapshots of the 7 incident personas (`lib/plan/goldenPlans.test.ts`, time frozen so the VDOT staleness discount can't drift them) plus direct assertions for the three defects that shipped — reaches race day, race on the correct weekday, no copy promising absent sessions, no placeholder leakage. Property sweep gains an HR dimension (absent / Tanaka / observed-low / max-only): 414,720 plans, up from 103,680 — the axis whose absence made F1 invisible. Two new invariants: `INV-PLAN-PEAK-IN-PEAK-PHASE` (warn when honestly labelled `maintenance`, error when the plan claims to be a build) and `INV-PLAN-NO-PLACEHOLDER-COPY` (warn). Verified by injecting a one-line config change — all 7 snapshots fail. |
| **Four coaching-rule corrections** (GEN-FIX-07, D1–D4) | **FREE** (D4 PAID-visible) | Wave 1b, 2026-08-06 | SLT-approved 2026-08-06. **D1** §2 — the 10% cap no longer applies to a post-deload bounceback (it made progressive overload arithmetically impossible in any plan with a recovery week). **D2** §79 — fitness assessed from VDOT *and* volume; lower level drives structure, higher drives intensity. **D3** §80 — finish-goal HM/marathon peak long run ≥70% of projected race *duration*, run-walk permitted explicitly, honest `long_run_shortfall_note` when the time cap binds. **D4** §81 — `compressed` split into `time_compressed` + `volume_constrained` (it was true for 5 of 6 personas and fed the PAID confidence score); `compressed` retained as a deprecated OR for one release, and the enricher now reads `time_compressed`. |
| **Week copy derived from session content** (GEN-FIX-06) | **FREE** (infra) | Wave 1b, 2026-08-06 | CoachingPrinciples §27. `weekLabel`/`weekTheme` now take a `WeekContent` summary (quality present? benchmark present? is this the volume peak?) instead of being pure functions of the phase name, so a week cannot be given copy promising sessions it does not contain. Closes F4 (beginners told "Build — first quality session" over three easy runs), F6 (no more invented `Target Race` / `Athlete` — empty, since every consumer already falls back), F9 (taper note counts the actual taper weeks instead of guessing from race distance). `INV-PLAN-THEME-MATCHES-PRESCRIPTION`'s four-literal denylist replaced by `INV-PLAN-COPY-MATCHES-SESSIONS`, which checks the **label as well as the theme** and asserts the rule rather than known-bad strings. |
| **Max HR plausibility gate** (GEN-FIX-05) | **FREE** (infra) | Wave 1b, 2026-08-06 | CoachingPrinciples §50 (amended). A supplied `max_hr` deviating >15% from Tanaka is not trusted — the engine falls back to the age estimate and surfaces a note naming both numbers plus the Profile override. Source-independent: a value inherited from `user_settings` carries no provenance and the harm is identical either way. New `GeneratorInput.max_hr_source: 'observed'` marks values the wizard read from HealthKit; those always surface a note even when plausible. `INV-PLAN-HR-ASSUMPTIONS-SURFACED` no longer exempts `karvonen` wholesale. **Closes the defect that gave the first organic user a Zone 2 ceiling 28 bpm low.** |
| ⚠️ **HealthKit-sourced max HR / resting HR** | FREE (infra) | *undocumented — added post-R24* | **Registry correction, 2026-08-06.** The row above described the R24 decision accurately but was never updated when the HealthKit paths landed, so the registry has been asserting "Tanaka" while the code has been using an observed value. **Four paths write HR:** (1) `GeneratePlanScreen` calls `fetchAppleHealthHRSnapshot()` **at generation time** and passes the result as `GeneratorInput.max_hr` — this bypasses Tanaka entirely; (2) `ConnectRunsScreen onHRFound` → `user_settings`; (3) `AppleHealthConnectionRow onHRFound` → `user_settings`; (4) manual Profile edit. Paths 1–3 are silent and only write values that are otherwise missing. **The derivation is unsafe**: `clientSync.ts:143` takes `max()` over 90 days of *any* HealthKit `heartRate` samples, including passive readings, and labels it physiological max — for a runner who has never recorded a maximal effort it is systematically far too low. Being fixed under **GEN-FIX-05** (`backlog.md` Wave 1b); evidence in `docs/incidents/2026-08-06-plan-defects/analysis.md` §6a. |

| Sign in with Apple | FREE (infra) | 2026-05-09 | Native iOS Sign in with Apple (closes Apple §5.1.1d for App Store submission, mandatory because Google OAuth is present). Plugin: `@capawesome/capacitor-apple-sign-in@0.1.0` — chosen over `@capacitor-community/apple-sign-in@7.1.0` because the latter is locked to `capacitor-swift-pm 7.x` and conflicts with `@capgo/capacitor-health@8.x` in Xcode SPM resolution. Login page (`app/auth/login/page.tsx`): `nonce → AppleSignIn.signIn({ scopes: [Email, FullName], nonce: sha256(rawNonce) }) → supabase.auth.signInWithIdToken({ provider:'apple', token, nonce: rawNonce }) → supabase.auth.updateUser({ data: { full_name } })` to capture Apple's first-auth-only `givenName`/`familyName`. Existing pre-fill in `DashboardClient.tsx:495–505` reads `user_metadata.full_name` and writes first/last to `user_settings`, same path Google uses. Web path falls back to `signInWithOAuth({ provider: 'apple' })`. Entitlement: `com.apple.developer.applesignin = ["Default"]` in `ios/App/App/App.entitlements`. Supabase Apple provider: `app.zonna.ios` in Authorized Client IDs (native-only path; no Services ID/.p8 key needed since `signInWithIdToken` verifies the token directly against Apple's public keys). **Don't switch back to `@capacitor-community/apple-sign-in`** — re-introduces the SPM conflict; CLAUDE.md has the warning baked in. **Gotcha (preserved at code level):** Apple returns name *only* on the very first authorization — the `updateUser({ data: { full_name } })` call is the single moment where it's persisted. Removing it leaves Profile blank forever with no recovery path. Simulator-validated 2026-05-09 (sign-in, name capture, persistence across re-auth). TestFlight smoke pending — non-blocking, no new code path. |
| HealthKit primary data source | FREE (infra) | 2026-05-09 | Apple Health replaces Strava as v1 primary data source. End-to-end pipeline now usable on iOS native. **Plugin:** `@capgo/capacitor-health@8.4.8` (cross-platform; HealthKit on iOS, Health Connect on Android). **Entitlement:** `com.apple.developer.healthkit = true` in `App.entitlements`; **Info.plist:** `NSHealthShareUsageDescription` ("Zonna reads your runs and recovery signals — resting heart rate, HRV, sleep — to provide coaching feedback. Zonna never writes to Apple Health."). Xcode App target has the HealthKit capability + `HealthKit.framework` linked (added 2026-05-09 — the entitlement key was set earlier but the framework wasn't linked, so runtime calls silently failed). Apple Dev portal HealthKit capability enabled 2026-05-08. **Migration:** `20260501_healthkit_primary.sql` — `source` + `apple_health_uuid` columns on `strava_activities` (NULLs-distinct unique constraints — Supabase upsert can't infer partial indexes); `health_daily_samples` table; `user_settings.healthkit_connected_at`; `session_completions.apple_health_uuid`; source-agnostic `run_analysis` IDs; `plan_adjustments.trigger_type` accepts `readiness_signal`. **Adapter:** `lib/health/adapter.ts` — pure HKWorkout + HR samples → `strava_activities` row mapper. **Sync:** `lib/health/clientSync.ts` — `requestHealthKitAuth` (workouts, HR, RHR, HRV, sleep, distance), `syncOnAppOpen` (foreground pull on app open: pages workouts since `lastSync`, posts to ingest API; pulls 14-day recovery samples, posts to samples API), `fetchAppleHealthHRSnapshot` for the pre-fill button. **APIs:** `POST /api/health/ingest` (workouts → `strava_activities` rows, mirrors Strava webhook enrichment pipeline), `POST /api/health/samples` (RHR/HRV/sleep → `health_daily_samples`). **UI:** Connect/disconnect row in MeScreen Profile (`AppleHealthRow` component, gated on `Capacitor.isNativePlatform()`); pre-fill button in HRZones section ("Use your Apple Health values" — populates RHR + observed MaxHR from last 90 days; partial-data tolerant: returns whichever of RHR/MaxHR exists, surfaces a soft message explaining which is still missing). **Downstream:** `lib/coaching/*` is source-agnostic (audited 2026-04-30 — every consumer reads `strava_activities` rows as a canonical internal shape, not a Strava-shaped contract); HealthKit provenance label on RunFeedbackCard (separately registered); VO2 max → race-times cross-check (`app/api/race-times/route.ts` `computeVO2CrossCheck`, ±10% divergence flag); readiness signal pre-session on hard days (`lib/coaching/planAdjustment.ts` `readinessSignal` input — RHR/HRV/sleep voting against 14-day baseline). `/privacy` page HealthKit disclosure (read-only, never written, on-device-only, revoke from iOS Settings or Profile). Feature gate `strava_intelligence` renamed to `activity_intelligence` in `lib/plan/featureGates.ts`. **Strava is now optional secondary** — backlog item `Strava as secondary source` covers the post-launch dedupe/merge work. Simulator-validated 2026-05-09 end-to-end (connect → permission grant → RHR sample read → pre-fill populates fields, with partial-data fix). TestFlight smoke (real watch streaming workouts) is non-blocking ceremony. |
| iOS push notifications (APNs wiring) | FREE (infra) | 2026-05-09 | iOS push delivery now live, completing the cross-platform push pipeline (web push via VAPID + iOS via APNs). Closes the layer-3 wiring left over from POST-RUN-01 (which was originally scheduled for shipping under POST-RUN-02 — now folded in). **Plugin:** `@capacitor/push-notifications@8.0.3`. **Entitlement:** `aps-environment` in `App.entitlements` (Xcode picks `development` for debug builds, `production` for archive builds — handled by the Push Notifications capability in the App target). Apple Dev portal Push Notifications capability enabled 2026-05-08; APNs .p8 key generated 2026-05-08. **Send pipeline:** `lib/apnpush.ts` builds an `apn.Provider` from env vars and ships notifications via the `apn` npm package; auto-cleanup on `BadDeviceToken`/`Unregistered` reasons. Vercel env vars: `APNS_KEY_ID`, `APNS_TEAM_ID`, `APNS_PRIVATE_KEY` (full .p8 including BEGIN/END headers), `APNS_TOPIC=app.zonna.ios`, `APNS_PRODUCTION` (`0` sandbox / `1` production — sandbox and production tokens are not interchangeable; flip when first TestFlight build goes up). **Subscribe pipeline:** `/api/push/subscribe` accepts both `{ platform: 'ios', token }` (raw APNs device token) and the VAPID-shape web push subscription; both write to `push_subscriptions` (migration `20260430_push_platform.sql` adds the `platform` column with a CHECK constraint). **Registration UI:** "Enable notifications" button in MeScreen (`enablePush` in `DashboardClient.tsx:5944`) — `requestPermissions → register → registration` listener captures token → POST to subscribe. Promise wrapped with a 30s timeout so the button can't hang forever when APNs sandbox is silently unreachable (a known simulator failure mode on older macOS/iOS combos); on timeout, logs to Xcode console and reverts to idle state. **Tap routing:** `pushNotificationActionPerformed` listener in `CapacitorBoot.tsx` reads `event.notification.data.url` and routes to it via `router.replace` — same `data.url` shape as web push (`/dashboard?screen=post-run&weekN=…&sessionDay=…` for the link-time post-run journey). **Send routes already in place:** `/api/push/send-weekly-report` (cron-driven weekly summary) and `lib/coaching/autoAnalyse.ts` (link-time post-run push from POST-RUN-01) both branch by `platform` and dispatch to either `sendWebPush` or `sendApnsPush`. Simulator-validated registration end-to-end 2026-05-09 — Zonna got a row in `push_subscriptions` with `platform='ios'` after Enable. Real-device delivery test deferred to first TestFlight build (simulator can't reliably receive live pushes). |
| AI coaching depth pass (AI-DEPTH-01) | PAID (infra) | 2026-05-11 | Closes three gaps surfaced by a manual coaching audit (Russ's Goring Gap 53km debrief vs. what Zonna would have produced). All three shipped in one commit. **(1) Athlete profile reaches every coaching prompt** — new `lib/coaching/prompts/athleteContext.ts` exports `buildAthleteContext({ plan })`. Surfaces captured traits (goal + target_time, fitness_level + training_age, hard_session_relationship, injury_history, terrain when non-default, days_available when ≤4). Block instructs implicit use, not list-back. Pre-this, the traits were used once at plan generation by `lib/plan/enrich.ts` and never reached the six in-life coaching surfaces. Wired into all six: daily coach note, session feedback, weekly report, phase summary, race readiness, plan-adjustment explanation. Empty string on legacy plans with no traits captured — graceful degradation. **(2) Weekly report names the worst session** — `pickSpotlightSession()` in `lib/coaching/weeklyReport.ts` selects the lowest `total_score` gated to verdicts `off_target`/`concerning` (reuses `VERDICT_BANDS` semantics — no duplicate threshold). Prompt block under data section instructs explicit naming ("Wednesday's easy run") and an anti-fabrication clause: only the numbers in the block, no invented paces/splits/HRs. Returns null on clean weeks so the model never fabricates a problem. No new schema or queries — uses fields already selected by `app/api/weekly-report/route.ts`. **(3) Synthesis surfaces upgraded to Sonnet 4.5** — `lib/ai/models.ts` split into `ANTHROPIC_MODEL` (claude-haiku-4-5-20251001, fast surfaces: daily coach note + session feedback) and `ANTHROPIC_MODEL_DEEP` (claude-sonnet-4-5-20250929, synthesis surfaces: weekly report + phase summary + race readiness + adjustment explanation). Plan enrichment (`lib/plan/enrich.ts`) stays on Haiku — call frequency too high to justify cost delta. `ai_model` audit columns on `weekly_reports`/`phase_summaries`/`race_readiness_notes`/`plan_adjustments` record actual model used per call so cost analysis stays accurate. **Not done in this pass:** image/vision analysis (privacy review needed), per-km splits + HR-over-time analysis (separate item AI-DEPTH-02), structured post-session interview (parked pending product decision — friction risk), multi-month trend block (AI-DEPTH-03), conversation memory (AI-DEPTH-04), post-race reshape (AI-DEPTH-08). |
| AI coaching depth pass II (AI-DEPTH-02a + AI-DEPTH-04 + spotlight standout) | PAID (infra) | 2026-05-11 | Second pass of audit-driven coaching improvements (shipped same day as AI-DEPTH-01). Three additions: **(1) Conversation memory (AI-DEPTH-04)** — `app/api/weekly-report/route.ts` now fetches the previous week's report (`weekly_reports` keyed on `(user_id, weekN - 1)`) in parallel with the existing data fetches and passes it to `buildWeeklyReportPrompt` as a `PreviousReportSummary` block. Prompt instructs the model to reference last week's coaching at most ONCE, only when this week's data tracks against it (improvement or repeat-issue). Week 1 and silent-fallback weeks (partial null fields) skip the fetch via conditional `Promise.resolve({ data: null })` so no I/O penalty when there's nothing to reference. Closes the "every report fires standalone, no continuity" audit gap. **(2) Spotlight standout (extension of AI-DEPTH-01)** — `pickSpotlightSession()` now falls back to picking the highest-scoring `verdict: 'nailed'` session when no concerning session exists, returning it with `polarity: 'standout'`. `SpotlightSession` gained a `polarity: 'concerning' \| 'standout'` field. Weekly report prompt renders a different naming rule per polarity: concerning = "what went wrong, name by day+type", standout = "set the bar / the one that mattered, one line of credit then move on". Gives clean weeks a positive anchor rather than a generic solid-week message. **(3) HR drift signal (AI-DEPTH-02 partial — AI-DEPTH-02a)** — new pure module `lib/coaching/streamAnalysis.ts` → `computeHrStreamSummary()` consumes `raw_payload.hrSamples` (HealthKit-only today; Strava doesn't yet persist per-sample HR) and returns `{ firstThirdAvgHr, lastThirdAvgHr, hrDriftBpm, hrDriftPct, samplesCount, coveragePerMin, sparse }`. Guards: returns null when duration < 10 min or either third has fewer than 3 samples. Wired into `app/api/analyse-run/route.ts` and passed to `buildSessionFeedbackPrompt` as a new `streamSummary` block. Prompt rule: reference drift directly when ≥ 10 bpm OR ≥ 7%; otherwise mention only if it's the dominant story. `sparse: true` flag (samples-per-min < 0.3) hedges the model's confidence — "treat as a hint, not a hard signal". **Still pending under AI-DEPTH-02b:** per-km splits analysis (HK bucket + Strava `splits_metric` ingest). No schema changes in this pass. |
| Cross-surface conversation memory (AI-DEPTH-10) | PAID (infra) | 2026-05-11 | Third pass of audit-driven coaching depth. Extends the AI-DEPTH-04 weekly-report continuity pattern to every other coaching surface so the AI now remembers what it said across the entire coaching arc. Four surfaces, one shape: each route fetches one prior artefact, passes it to the prompt as a labelled block, prompt enforces "reference at most once, only when this surface's data tracks against it." **(1) Daily coach note ← last weekly report.** `app/api/daily-coach-note/route.ts` fetches `weekly_reports` (most recent row with non-null headline) and passes `previousWeeklyReport: { headline, body }` into `buildDailyCoachNotePrompt`. Lets the morning note connect today's session to the week's coaching story (e.g. weekly said "easy ran hot" → daily on an easy day says "hold the zone today"). **(2) Plan adjustment ← previous resolved adjustment.** `app/api/adjust-plan/route.ts` fetches the most recent `plan_adjustments` row with status in `('confirmed', 'auto_applied', 'reverted')` (deliberately excluding 'pending' — that's the current proposal) and passes `previousAdjustment: { summary, triggerType, adjustmentType, status, daysAgo }` to `buildAdjustmentExplanationPrompt` as a 3rd arg. New `PreviousAdjustmentSummary` type. Lets the new explanation acknowledge continuity ("second time this month") or contradiction ("last time we softened the long run, this time we're swapping intervals"). **(3) Phase summary ← previous phase summary.** `app/api/phase-summary/route.ts` fetches the most recent `phase_summaries` row with `transition_week_n < current` and passes `previousPhaseSummary: { phaseEnded, content }` into `buildPhaseSummaryPrompt`. Lets the new summary frame trajectory across transitions — zone discipline that improved across base→build, fitness that built then sharpened, etc. **(4) Session feedback ← last same-type analysed session.** `app/api/analyse-run/route.ts` fetches the user's 20 most recent `run_analysis` rows, walks them looking up each one's session.type from the plan (`plan.weeks[n].sessions[dayKey]`), and picks the first match that isn't the current row being analysed (matched by `(week_n, session_day)`). Distinct from the R25 averaged cohort — this is a single specific prior run the model can name by day + days-ago. Passes `previousSimilarSession: { dayLabel, daysAgo, verdict, hrInZonePct }` into `buildSessionFeedbackPrompt`. Lets feedback say "Tuesday's easy nailed, this one drifted" or "second easy in a row that ran hot". No schema changes — every fetch reuses existing tables and is added to the route's existing `Promise.all` batch (zero extra round trips). All four continuity blocks render only when prior data exists; never fabricate context on first-run scenarios. Closes the audit gap "every prompt fires standalone, no memory of prior outputs" completely across every AI coaching surface in the app. |
| Recent tweaks log on MeScreen (PROFILE-ADJ-02) | PAID | 2026-05-12 | "Recent tweaks" card on MeScreen surfacing the last 5 silent/auto-applied plan adjustments (zone drift, RPE disconnect / effort signal, shadow load, readiness signal — anything written with `status='auto_applied'`). Closes the visibility gap left by PROFILE-ADJ-01: the engine could silently update the plan and users had no way to see what changed. Read-only by design — the pending-adjustment card at the top of the section remains the only tappable adjustment surface. **Fetch:** `DashboardClient.tsx` `Promise.all` block adds `supabase.from('plan_adjustments').select('id, trigger_type, summary, created_at, week_n').eq('user_id').eq('status', 'auto_applied').order('created_at', { ascending: false }).limit(5)`. Result stored as `recentAutoAdjustments` state, passed to `MeScreen` as a new prop. **Render:** Inserted between the "Last checked" status block and the "Check now" button in the existing Plan adjustments card. Each row shows a human label (`Zone drift` / `Effort signal` / `Shadow load` / `Load spike` / `Efficiency dip` / `Manual`, mapped from `trigger_type`), a relative time (same vocab as `lastCheckedLabel`: just now / today / yesterday / N days ago / last week / N weeks ago), and the engine's `summary` text verbatim. Hidden when array is empty — the "Last checked" line already covers the "engine has run, nothing to show" case. Forward-compat: unknown `trigger_type` values fall back to "Plan tweak" so new triggers don't render broken UI before the label map is updated. |
| Plan schema additive fields (AI-DEPTH-07) | FREE (infra) | 2026-05-12 | Schema-only extension reserved for AI-DEPTH-08 (post-race reshape). Adds three optional fields to `Session` — `key_session: boolean` (marks pivotal sessions: long runs, race-pace tempos, planned tune-ups), `run_walk_strategy: string` (e.g. "9:1 from km 10"), `fueling_protocol: string` (e.g. "gel every 25 min from km 10") — and one optional field to `Week`: `result_embedded: RaceResult \| null` (race-day outcome captured into the plan on the race week). New `RaceResult` interface in `types/plan.ts` covering both auto-populatable telemetry (`finish_time`, `distance_km`, `date`, `splits`, `avg_hr`, `max_hr`, `hr_drift_pct`, `rpe`, `outcome` enum) and runner-written reflection (`notes`, `fueling_outcome`, `strategy_outcome`, `what_worked`, `what_broke`). Mirrored in `lib/plan/schema.ts` Zod (new `RaceResultSchema` + `RaceResultSchemaType` export). `tune_up_callout` and `lr_segment_pace` also surfaced to Zod (were in TS but missing from schema). Documented in `docs/canonical/plan-schema.md`. **Engine does not populate these yet** — AI-DEPTH-08 will write `result_embedded` post-event and propose `fueling_protocol` / `run_walk_strategy` / `key_session` updates from race-day signal. **Audit pass clean across every plan consumer:** enricher `mergePlan()` does field-by-field copy on a deep clone of the original (new fields survive enrichment); `savePlanForUser` is plain JSONB upsert (no field stripping); no `.strict()` Zod usage anywhere (extra fields would pass through even if a tool hadn't been updated to know about them); no blind `Object.keys`/`Object.assign` patterns on sessions in `lib/plan/*` or `app/api/*`. No Supabase migration — `plans.plan_json` is already JSONB and accepts new fields automatically. |
| Subscription terms disclosure (Apple §3.1.2(a)) | FREE (infra) | 2026-05-12 | Strengthened the legal disclosure block on `app/dashboard/UpgradeScreen.tsx` from the previous one-line "Auto-renews. Cancel any time. 14-day free trial included on first subscription." to a full Apple §3.1.2(a)-compliant block: per-period prices (sourced from `PRICING.monthly.label` / `PRICING.annual.label` so a price change in `lib/brand.ts` flows through), explicit trial conversion behaviour ("first payment charged at the end of the trial unless you cancel"), the mandated "Payment is charged to your Apple ID at confirmation of purchase" boilerplate, renewal terms ("auto-renews ... unless turned off at least 24 hours before renewal"), cancellation path ("Manage or cancel any time in your Apple ID account settings"), and functional links to `/terms` + `/privacy` opening in new tabs. Brand voice is allowed to break in this block — it's a compliance surface, not a coaching surface. Also fixed two stale `https://zona.app/privacy` and `https://zona.app/terms` hardcoded URLs in `app/auth/login/page.tsx` (lines 374, 393), switching to relative paths so a future custom-domain change leaves no stale strings. This was a launch-blocker for App Store submission. |
| Strava OAuth on native | FREE (infra) | 2026-05-12 | Port of the Strava connect flow from `window.location.href` (full-page redirect, can't return to a native app) to the SFSafariViewController + custom URL scheme pattern that Google OAuth already uses on iOS. Four files: **(1)** `app/api/strava/connect/route.ts` accepts a new `?platform=ios` query param and encodes it into Strava's opaque OAuth `state` as `"userId\|ios"`. Bare userId state remains valid for backward-compat web flows. **(2)** `app/api/strava/callback/route.ts` decodes both state shapes, then routes the success/failure redirect through a new `buildRedirect()` helper: native flows go to `app.zonna.ios://strava-callback?strava=<status>`, web flows keep the existing `${origin}/dashboard?strava=<status>` path. Helper consolidates the four redirect call-sites (denied / error / upgrade / connected). **(3)** `StravaConnectionRow` connect button in `DashboardClient.tsx` dynamic-imports `@capacitor/core` + `@capacitor/browser`; on native it opens the connect URL via `Browser.open({ presentationStyle: 'popover' })` with the `platform=ios` flag, on web it keeps the legacy full-page redirect. Dynamic import keeps the web bundle from paying for native shims. **(4)** `components/CapacitorBoot.tsx` `appUrlOpen` listener gains a `strava-callback` branch — closes the SFSafariViewController via `Browser.close()` and routes to `/dashboard?strava=<status>` so the existing `StravaConnectionRow` `useEffect` handles the result (no new UI path needed; the result UI is identical to the web flow). Custom URL scheme `app.zonna.ios://` is already registered in `ios/App/App/Info.plist` for the Google OAuth flow — no additional Info.plist config needed. Closes the last remaining backlog blocker in NOW group B that was buildable in-codebase. |
| Post-run journey resolves at the read (POST-RUN-02) | TIER-DIVERGENT | 2026-05-14 | Closes the gap between "the app could do this" and "the user feels it work" in the auto-link + post-run flow. Three surfaces, one commit. **(1) Session card CTA is auto-match-aware.** In `SessionPopupInner` (`app/dashboard/DashboardClient.tsx` ~line 2722) when `autoMatchedActivity` is non-null, the primary CTA renders as a moss-filled "Log this run" with an `AIMark` subline showing `{activity name} · {distance} · {timeAgo}` (new `formatRelativeTime()` helper near `formatRaceCountdown()` returns "this morning" / "earlier today" / "this evening" / "yesterday" / "Nd ago"; `null` past 6 days or for future dates so the subline gracefully drops out). A tertiary "Wrong one? Pick another" reopens the picker via `setView('complete')`. The old "Match a Strava run" wording is retained only for the no-auto-match path — accurate to what the button does. Closes the wording bug where `handleMarkComplete` (POST-RUN-01) was already silently skipping the picker on high-confidence matches but the button still read as if the user was about to be sent to one. **(2) PostRunScreen header reflects analysis state (PAID only).** New subtitle line below the eyebrow: pending → `<AIMark working />` + "Reading the run…"; ready → `<AIMark />` + "Here's the read."; gave-up → "Logged. Tell me how it felt." Gated on `hasPaidAccess` because free users don't get the AI verdict at all — showing them "Reading the run…" would be a lie. Replaces the previously silent gap during the analysis poll window. **(3) Done routes to `SessionScreen`, not Today.** New `onDone` prop on `PostRunScreen`. Parent handler at `screen === 'post-run'` route (line ~1226) merges the freshest `allCompletions[weekN][sessionKey]` into the session, sets `activeSessionData`, and routes to `'session'` so the verdict (which `SessionScreen` already renders inline) is the resting state — not the screen the user started on. Back arrow still routes to Today. **Polish:** new `onAnalysisLoaded` callback lifts the locally-polled `run_analysis` row into the parent's `runAnalysisMap` (`setRunAnalysisMap(prev => ({ ...prev, [sessionDay]: row }))`) so the Done → SessionScreen transition renders the verdict immediately instead of triggering SessionScreen's own 2.5s poll for data already in hand. **Push notification copy (lib/coaching/autoAnalyse.ts):** title rewritten from "Run linked." to `{Day}'s {distLabel}.` (e.g. "Tuesday's 6.2K."); body rewritten to "How did it feel?" — mirrors PostRunScreen's "How did it feel?" RPE-prompt header so the tap-through reads as continuation, not a new question. Tier: TIER-DIVERGENT — auto-match CTA works for all users; analysis-state subtitle is gated on `hasPaidAccess`. ~180 LOC across `app/dashboard/DashboardClient.tsx` + `lib/coaching/autoAnalyse.ts`. No schema changes. |

| Hold the zone — brand visibility across the app (HOLD-THE-ZONE-01) | FREE | 2026-05-14 | Makes the BRAND.voiceAnchor (`"Hold the zone"`) visible across the product. Audit found the phrase was on the marketing site and inside AI-generated coach copy only — zero in-product UI surfaces used it as a visible label. The app is named Zonna; zone discipline is the USP; yet zones were a chip everywhere and a headline nowhere. **Foundation:** New `components/shared/ZoneBar.tsx` — 5-segment visual primitive (one segment filled in zone colour from session-type tokens), optional `showLabels` for Session Detail variant. Helper `zoneNumberForType(type) → 1\|2\|3\|4\|5` distinct from existing `zoneForSessionType` in `lib/coaching/zoneRules.ts` (which returns a `ZoneBand` object grouping 4+5 as `'Z4-5'`); the visual bar wants all five segments distinct so intervals (Z5) is visually separate from race (Z4). **(1) OrientationScreen zone intro.** Replaces the single `"Your Zone 2 ceiling is X bpm"` line with the full 5-zone breakdown using the user's personal HR ranges (from existing `calculateZones(rhr, mhr)` + `ZONE_DEFS`). First visible use of `"Hold the zone"` anywhere in the product UI — eyebrow with pulsing moss dot. Headline `"These are your zones."` ("your" in moss). Zone 2 row gets a `"Your home"` tag naming where most training lives. Brand-position callout: `"Easy when it's easy. Hard when it's hard. The grey middle is where amateurs go to stall."` CTA renamed `"Start training"` → `"I'm ready"`. Graceful fallback when HR isn't set: zone names visible, HR ranges show em-dash, prompts to set HR in Profile. **(2) TodayScreen.** `"Hold the zone · Zone N today"` eyebrow inserted between hero and the `"Today's session"` section label, with a pulsing moss dot; renders only when today is a zone-bearing run session (skipped on rest / strength / cross-train). Plus a tiny 4px ZoneBar strip (no labels — glance-only) below the SessionCard. The hero `"10km, slowly."` is deliberately UNCHANGED — `"slowly"` can be felt, `"Zone 2"` has to be learned. Layered brand: hero owns the experience, eyebrow + bar carry the prescription. **(3) Session Detail prescription card.** Replaces the small 11px zone chip above the metric grid with a prescription card: `"Hold the zone"` eyebrow (pulse dot) + 22px `"Zone N · {short name}"` (e.g. `"Zone 2 · aerobic"`) in the zone colour + HR range from `getSessionHRDisplay()` + labelled `<ZoneBar showLabels />` at the bottom. Session Detail exists to sell the prescription; this makes the prescription the biggest element on the screen after the title. **(4) MeScreen ordering.** Promoted `HRZonesSection` (and the `!hrConfigured` warning) above the Plan/benchmark actions card. Zones — the core product concept — now sit prominently in `"Your training"` section rather than buried below settings. **SLC discipline:** SessionCard component itself is unchanged (no chips added inside); new surfaces layer around it. Today's ZoneBar has no number labels — labels live only on Session Detail where the prescription deserves the focus. Hold-the-zone eyebrow is conditional on zone-bearing session types — no clutter on rest days. Audit + design rationale in `mockups/hold-the-zone-audit.html` (side-by-side Before/After, visibility ranking table, ship-order recommendations). ~290 LOC across `app/dashboard/DashboardClient.tsx` + new `components/shared/ZoneBar.tsx`. No schema changes. |
| Session Detail consolidation (SESSION-DETAIL-CONSOLIDATE-01) | FREE | 2026-05-14 | Tightens the Session Detail screen after HOLD-THE-ZONE-01 added the prescription card on top of the older metric-grid layout — leaving HR/zone rendered two or three times in the top viewport, and the brand-defining "Why this session" block sitting fifth from the top. Three passes, one commit, on `app/dashboard/DashboardClient.tsx` → `SessionPopupInner`. **(1) Kill the duplicate HR + add the voice line.** Removed the HR tile from the metric grid (prescription card already owns Zone + HR + ZoneBar — same number, same chip, same data); grid is now 2-up Distance/Duration + Pace. The prescription card itself is now the tap target for `ZoneInfoSheet` — whole card is a `<button>`, ⓘ affordance added to the eyebrow row (matches ui-patterns.md §19 drill-down pattern). Tap-to-learn behaviour preserved exactly; the surface moved from the deleted HR tile up onto the prescription card. New helper `getSessionVoiceLine(sessionType)` returns a one-sentence session-type-aware voice line ("Conversational the whole way. If you can talk, you're in." on easy; "Hours, not effort. Slower than you think you should." on long; "Hard on the reps. Easy on the rests. Don't blur it." on intervals; etc. — 8 lines covering easy/run/long/quality/tempo/intervals/hard/race/recovery/strength/rest). Rendered between prescription and metric grid, `13px 400 --ink-2`. Same job as Today's hero ("10km, slowly.") — voice anchor in the moment. **(2) Reorder + simplify.** WHY THIS SESSION moved from below all execution detail up to directly under the prescription/voice/metric block — brand-defining content reads first, then "how" (structure / description). Description block ("What to do") is now suppressed when the structured composer renders — same instruction, two formats was the original duplication; composer wins, description is the fallback only for race / rest / strength shapes or sessions without a catalogue row. Week focus block removed from Session Detail entirely (plan-level statement, lives on Plan / Today). "How did it feel?" (RPE + body feeling) promoted above the sticky CTA when complete or skipped — was rendered below the CTA, which inverted the layout (the reflective state should outrank the corrective "Update log" action). Auto-matched CTA secondary actions collapsed from 5 visible tap targets (primary + match subline + Wrong one? + Log manually + Skip) to 1 dominant primary + 1 inline text-link row separated by `·` ("Wrong one? · Log manually · Skip" in `--mute`, smaller font). Function preserved, visual hierarchy fixed. **(3) Warm Slate cleanup on metric grid + execution summary.** System B aliases swept: `--bg` → `--card`, `--border-col` → `--line`, `--text-{primary,secondary,muted}` → `--ink` / `--ink-2` / `--mute`, `--accent-soft` / `--teal*` → `--moss` / `--moss-soft` / `--moss-mid`, `--amber*` → `--warn` / `--warn-bg`, `0.5px solid` → `1px solid`. Done/Skipped status pills in the top block also brought onto Warm Slate. Pace tile drops the "HR-derived estimate" caption (utility-speak; renders only "Pace target" when the value came from the plan). Pace skeleton tile removed — when pace isn't ready yet, the tile renders nothing rather than a pulsing empty placeholder. **Long sessions** restored to the metric-grid render allowlist (a regression in the original code excluded `'long'` from the type list — now `['easy','run','quality','intervals','hard','tempo','long','race','recovery']`). Audit + design rationale in `mockups/session-detail-audit.html` (side-by-side Before/After phone preview, voice line table by session type, ship-order). ~250 LOC delta on `SessionPopupInner`, net deletion. No schema changes, no new components. |
| Plan screen redesign (PLAN-REDESIGN-01) | FREE | 2026-05-14 | Closes the Phase-3 consistency gap on the Plan screen — last major surface still flat and silent. Three passes, one commit. **(1) Plan voice block.** Inline rule-engine voice card above the calendar in `PlanScreen` (`app/dashboard/DashboardClient.tsx`), with a 3px moss left rail (coaching-surface signal), "THIS WEEK" eyebrow + phase chip on right, one-sentence headline, up to 2 items. Helpers `buildWeekVoiceContext` / `getWeekVoiceHeadline` / `getWeekVoiceItems` extracted to module-level so the Coach screen's `PlanCoachingCard` shares the same derivation. No `CoachByline` / `AIMark` — rule-engine output per the provenance rule (same treatment as Today's plan-coach-note). AI-generated variant (PLAN-VOICE-AI) deferred to backlog; this surface stays put when that lands, only the data source changes. **(2) Token migration on PlanCalendar.tsx.** PlanCalendar was the last major surface still on System B aliases. 38 token references migrated to Warm Slate: `--card-bg` → `--card`, `--border-col` → `--line`, `--text-{primary,secondary,muted}` → `--ink` / `--ink-2` / `--mute`, `--accent` → `--moss`, `--teal-{soft,mid,dim}` → `--moss-{soft,mid,mid}`. `0.5px solid` borders → `1px solid`. Hardcoded `14px` border-radius → `var(--radius-lg)`. Container horizontal padding `12px` → `16px` to match canonical content padding (cross-screen consistency table). **Visual hierarchy:** current-week metric pair bumped 18px → 36px (800 weight, -0.02em tracking, tabular-nums); next/later weeks stay at 18px. Race week now gets a 3px `--s-race` left rail + "Race week · " eyebrow prefix in the calendar (was indistinguishable from any other week — PlanArc already marks it `--s-race`, the two views now agree). Move-mode hint copy: "tap an empty day to move, or another session to swap" → "Tap where you want it." (brand voice: one sentence). **(3) Section structure + `WeekStripCard` (new).** Calendar restructured from a flat list of weeks into four named sections: **Past** (collapsible, strip cards when expanded), **Now** (full WeekCard, dominant 36px metric), **Next** (full WeekCard, calmer 18px metric), **Later** (strip cards for all remaining weeks). New `WeekStripCard` component alongside `WeekCard` in `components/training/PlanCalendar.tsx` — compressed weekly summary: 7 status dots (●/○/⊘/—) in a single row, header with week label + total km, race-week tag at the bottom with the actual race date (`weekday, day month`). Reclaims ~80% vertical space across a 16-week plan. New `PlanSectionLabel` helper renders the Past/Now/Next/Later headers (`11px 700 --mute uppercase 0.12em`, optional right-aligned count for "Later"). **Limitation on this pass:** strip cards are read-only. Move/swap restricted to current + next weeks (the typical adjustment window — sessions ≥3 weeks out are rarely moved in practice). Tap-to-expand for strip cards is a deferred enhancement; if a user needs to move a session in a Later week, they can wait until that week becomes Next. Audit mockup at `mockups/plan-screen-audit.html` documents the design rationale, side-by-side Before/After phone preview, token migration table, ship-order. ~480 LOC across `app/dashboard/DashboardClient.tsx` + `components/training/PlanCalendar.tsx`. No schema changes. |
| Medium-confidence Strava match CTA (AUTO-MATCH-02) | PAID | 2026-05-16 | Closes the "I ran but the app says nothing" UX hole left by POST-RUN-01 / POST-RUN-02. Both prior passes only surfaced a CTA at *high* confidence (≥70, score = same-day + distance + effort signals); medium candidates (≥40) were dropped on the floor, leaving users with a session card that gave no breadcrumb that a plausible run had even been seen. Doctrine of the silent server auto-link is unchanged ("wrong link is worse than a picker tap" — `lib/coaching/autoAnalyse.ts:81–84` still high-only). What changed is the *client-side* surface, where a tap is itself a confirmation. **Code path:** `activeAutoMatch` in `DashboardClient.tsx` (~line 951) widened to return `{ activity, confidence: 'high' \| 'medium' } \| null` instead of `StravaActivity \| null`; prop renamed `autoMatchedActivity → autoMatch` across `SessionScreen` and `SessionPopupInner` (single-file rename, internal API only — no contract doc touched, `docs/contracts/components/session-card.md` references the components by name but never the prop). `handleMarkComplete` consumes `autoMatch.activity` the same way regardless of tier. **Visual branch in the CTA block** (`SessionPopupInner` ~line 2983): `confidence === 'high'` keeps the existing filled-moss "Log this run" + `AIMark` label "Strava run matched by Zonna" — pixel-for-pixel unchanged. `confidence === 'medium'` renders an outline-moss button ("Looks like this one?" — phrased as a question) with `AIMark` label "Possible Strava match"; subline (`{name} · {distance} · {timeAgo}`) and three-link escape row ("Wrong one? · Log manually · Skip") are identical to the high branch. The visible run name + distance + timestamp make the tap an explicit confirmation either way — same handler, same downstream POST-RUN-02 routing. `low` candidates are still suppressed (too noisy). **Tier rationale:** PAID because the underlying Strava ingestion + auto-match pipeline is paid-only per the registry's existing Strava integration row; this is an enhancement of the visibility layer on top of an already-paid feature. Free users see no change. **No schema, no migration.** ~30 LOC delta on `app/dashboard/DashboardClient.tsx`. Originated from a UX dig with user (2026-05-16) — alongside POST-LOG-01. |
| Collapse SessionPopupInner on logged sessions (POST-LOG-01) | FREE | 2026-05-16 | Closes the "session screen is too noisy once I've logged" complaint. Post-log SessionScreen was stacking `RunFeedbackCard` (verdict + scores + AI prose) on top of the full pre-run `SessionPopupInner` — Plan vs Actual, Why this session, warmup/main/cooldown structure, the RPE form — so the headline (the verdict) had to share oxygen with reference material the user no longer needed in their face. **Behaviour:** `SessionScreen` now renders a collapsible wrapper around `SessionPopupInner`. When `session.completion?.status === 'complete'` the body is collapsed by default behind a single eyebrow-styled toggle row ("Session details · tweak how it felt" / "Hide session details") with a chevron that rotates 180° on expand. Pre-log / future / skipped sessions render expanded — behaviour unchanged for those states. **Layout:** toggle is full-width, 14px×18px padding, `1px var(--line)` divider visible only when open, 44px min tap-target. Eyebrow uses canonical section-label tokens (`10px 700 var(--mute) uppercase 0.08em`) per ui-patterns.md typography scale. SVG chevron uses `currentColor → var(--mute)`. Whole left-rail card structure preserved — colour rail + radius + border identical to before. **Regression guard (subtle):** the `useEffect` that re-inits `briefOpen` keys on `session.weekN` + `session.key` only — NOT on `isComplete`. Rationale: when a user logs the active session mid-screen, `saveCompletion` writes to DB, parent refetches, `session.completion` populates, `isComplete` flips true — but `SessionPopupInner` is right then transitioning into its own `'reflect'` view for inline RPE/fatigue entry. Recollapsing on the `isComplete` flip would tear the reflect form out from under the user's fingers. Keying on session identity instead means the collapse only fires when the user navigates between sessions (e.g. tapping a different day) — never mid-interaction on the current one. **Tier:** FREE — pure UI affordance, applies to all users. **No schema, no migration, no contract change** (collapse state is local to `SessionScreen`). ~80 LOC delta on `app/dashboard/DashboardClient.tsx`. Originated from a UX dig with user (2026-05-16) — alongside AUTO-MATCH-02. |
| LaunchScreen solid Warm Slate (LAUNCH-SCREEN-01) | FREE (infra) | 2026-05-16 | Fixes the two-stage boot flicker reported on TestFlight: the iOS native `LaunchScreen.storyboard` rendered an oversized concentric-circles + "Zonna" PNG (`Splash.imageset/Default@*.png`, 1366×1366) under `contentMode="scaleAspectFill"`, which spilled past iPhone screen edges and then crossfaded into a *completely different* web loading screen (Warm Slate bg + `<Wordmark />` + `BRAND.tagline`) once the React app mounted. Two visual languages back-to-back — circles vs no circles, baked PNG vs live Inter text, different vertical centring — made the boot feel broken. **Fix:** `ios/App/App/Base.lproj/LaunchScreen.storyboard` reduced to a single `UIView` with `backgroundColor` set to `#F3F0EB` (Warm Slate `--bg`, matching the web loading screen and `capacitor.config.ts → backgroundColor` exactly). No image view, no label, no circles. Because Capacitor's iOS splash-screen plugin reuses the same LaunchScreen storyboard for its post-launch overlay (it calls `UIStoryboard(name: "LaunchScreen")` under the hood), this one change kills both the OS-level launch screen circles AND the Capacitor splash overlay circles — they're sourced from the same place. **Why solid colour instead of native wordmark+tagline:** the original backlog spec offered a wordmark/tagline variant that mirrored the web loading screen pixel-for-pixel. Rejected because (1) it'd require re-exporting a wordmark PNG and re-archiving on every brand rename ("brand may change again" per CLAUDE.md) while the web loading screen reads `BRAND.name` / `BRAND.tagline` from `lib/brand.ts` live, (2) it only buys ~200ms of "same image stays put" before the web loading screen swaps in, (3) Apple reviewers don't grade launch-screen content, only that it doesn't look like a hang. Solid colour gives a seamless boot: native renders Warm Slate rectangle → web loading screen fades in with wordmark + tagline → app. **Cleanup deferred:** the orphaned `Splash.imageset` directory (~840KB across three PNG scales) is no longer referenced by the storyboard but still lives in `Assets.xcassets`. Removing it cleanly would require touching `ios/App/App.xcodeproj/project.pbxproj` to drop the asset reference, which is high-risk to hand-edit; the dead weight is harmless. **Manual step required:** the user (or whoever holds Xcode signing certs) must Archive + upload to TestFlight from Xcode — storyboard changes are baked into the iOS bundle and don't propagate over-the-air. `npx cap sync ios` already ran on commit. Removes the backlog item under NOW.B. |
