AI failure silently degrades back to rule-engine (ADR-006). **New surface:** `POST /api/plan-weekly-note` — mirrors the `phase-summary` route template (auth → tier check → idempotency lookup → parallel fetch of plan + settings + previous-week note → AI call → parse → insert). Output contract is structured text (`HEADLINE: …` + `ITEM: …` lines) parsed by `parsePlanWeeklyNoteResponse` in `lib/coaching/prompts/planWeeklyNote.ts`; unparseable response → 503 → client treats as 'failed' → rule-engine fallback. **New table:** `plan_weekly_notes` (PK `user_id, week_n`; columns `headline`, `items JSONB`, `generated_at`, `ai_model`; RLS read/insert/delete own). Migration `20260520_plan_weekly_notes.sql`. **Continuity (AI-DEPTH-04/10):** the most recent prior week's note from this table feeds the prompt with the canonical "reference at most once when this week tracks against it" rule, so the week-card story carries across weeks rather than firing standalone. **Cache invalidation:** any plan save invalidates all of the user's weekly notes — `savePlanForUser` (`lib/plan.ts`) now appends a `DELETE FROM plan_weekly_notes WHERE user_id = ?` after the plan upsert. Covers all writes through one path: plan regeneration, R20 reshape, confirmed/reverted adjustments, zone recalibration. The next Plan-screen view regenerates lazily against the new session shape — prevents the brand-destroying failure mode where Kit narrates "Tomorrow's tempo run" against a regenerated week that has no tempo run. **Prompt builder:** `lib/coaching/prompts/planWeeklyNote.ts` — composes `buildVoiceHeader` + `buildAthleteContext` + week session list (Mon–Sun, type + distance, rest days skipped) + phase + race countdown + previous-week continuity block. **Client (PlanScreen):** new state `aiNote: { headline, items } \| 'loading' \| 'failed' \| null`; `useEffect` keyed on `(hasPaidAccess, currentWeekIndex, plan)` fires `authedFetch('/api/plan-weekly-note', { week_n })` for paid users with an in-flight cancel guard. The existing IIFE render block in `DashboardClient.tsx` now branches: paid-ready → CoachByline + AI headline/items; paid-loading → CoachByline `working` + skeleton lines matching the final shape (no reflow, no spinner per ui-patterns.md); free or paid-failed → rule-engine voice (existing path, unchanged). New props on `PlanScreen`: `hasPaidAccess: boolean`, `onOpenCoach?: () => void`; wired through from `DashboardClient`. **Pattern 24 doc** (ui-patterns.md) rewritten to document the four-row tier matrix; the doc's "provenance rule (critical)" section was previously deferring this work — now reflects shipped state. ~250 LOC across 4 new files + edits to `DashboardClient.tsx` + `lib/plan.ts`. Originated as PLAN-REDESIGN-01's deferred PLAN-VOICE-AI item. |

| Post-run reflection + AI reframe (POST-RUN-REFRAME-01) | PAID | 2026-05-22 | The differentiator. After a tough session, the runner can write a reflection on the post-run reflect view (`SessionScreen` reflect view + `PostRunScreen` — both paths wired) and Kit returns a 3-4 sentence reframe with the **hug AND the truth**: acknowledge → cause → progress → race anchor. Warmth-as-permission in sentence 1 (*"You're allowed a bad one"*), never cheerleading. **Data-source ladder** — works for any user regardless of Strava/HealthKit: Tier A (≥12 activities in 84 days) gets cohort + multi-month trend + HR drift; Tier B (≥6 completions in 28 days, no activity feed) gets plan completion + RPE pattern; Tier C (new user) gets phase position + sessions logged. Tier detection: `lib/coaching/reframeTier.ts → detectReframeTier()`. Thresholds in `lib/coaching/constants.ts → REFRAME_TIER`. **Risk gate** — reframe is silenced when any of four signals fire: current session `coaching_flag === 'flag'`, ≥2 flags in last 5 completions, ≥3 consecutive Heavy/Wrecked tags, or HR drift ≥15 bpm/≥10% on this session. Silenced response surfaces the coaching warning instead (amber-rail card, no `AIMark` — rule-engine output, not model). Logic: `lib/coaching/reframeRiskGate.ts → assessReframeRiskGate()`; reasons: `session_flagged`, `repeated_overload`, `fatigue_accumulation`, `severe_hr_drift`. Unit-tested via `lib/coaching/reframeRiskGate.test.ts` (16 cases). **Multi-month trend (AI-DEPTH-03 bundled)** — `lib/coaching/runHistory.ts → buildHrTrendSeries()` buckets same-effort runs by month, returns HR/pace deltas with threshold gates (≥4 bpm or ≥5 s/km to be cited). Standalone endpoint `GET /api/coaching/trend` for future Coach-screen consumers; in-route inline call for the reframe Tier A branch. **Prompt** — `lib/coaching/prompts/sessionReframe.ts → buildSessionReframePrompt()`, version `1.0.0`, uses `ANTHROPIC_MODEL_DEEP` (Sonnet) — reframe-with-cohort reasoning is closer to plan-shaped reasoning than to short single-shot feedback. Voice header reuses `buildVoiceHeader` + 5 inline few-shot examples derived from the golden cases. Cheerleader-word rejection regex on the response (`amazing|crushing|smash|beast mode|you've got this|crushed|don't give up`) — bad outputs nullify and the route returns `fallback: true`. **Storage** — new `session_reflections` table (migration `20260522_session_reflections.sql`), composite key `(user_id, week_n, session_day)`, columns for user input (`note_text`, `note_source`, voice metadata) + AI output (`reframe_text`, `reframe_data_tier`, `reframe_model`, `reframe_prompt_version`, `reframe_silenced`, `reframe_silenced_reason`, `reframe_generated_at`). RLS read/insert/update/delete own. Voice metadata columns are pre-wired (no schema change needed for POST-RUN-REFRAME-02). **Route** — `app/api/post-run-reframe/route.ts`, PAID via new `post_run_reframe` gate in `lib/plan/featureGates.ts → PAID_ONLY_ONGOING`. **UI** — shared `components/training/ReflectionInput.tsx`, hydrates from existing reflection on mount (re-entering the surface shows the prior reframe or silenced warning, not a blank input), four view states (`input` / `submitting` / `reframe` / `silenced`), AIMark mandatory on reframe card with 3px moss left-rail + "Kit" eyebrow. Wired into both `SessionScreen` reflect view (manual completion path) and `PostRunScreen` (Strava-linked path) in `app/dashboard/DashboardClient.tsx`, gated on `hasPaidAccess && rpe !== null` so reflection comes after structured inputs. **Voice deferred** — POST-RUN-REFRAME-02 in backlog (gated on OpenAI Whisper vendor decision + Capacitor mic plugin + device testing). **Doctrine** — new §60 in `CoachingPrinciples.md`. **Voice spec** — locked register + 3 good / 4 bad examples in `brand.md § Reframe Voice` (the only Zonna surface that extends beyond one sentence; structure is the discipline). **Golden suite** — 4 cases in `docs/canonical/reframe-golden-cases.md`: Case A (Tier A, real user data), Case B (Tier B), Case C (Tier C, new user), Case D (risk-gate silenced). |

New `ShareWeekButton` component sits next to "Generate report"/"Refresh" inside the weekly report card on `CoachScreen`, rendered only when the current week has a `zone_discipline_score`. Free CoachTeaser gains a second upsell card ("Share your week") below the existing zone-score teaser — same `onUpgrade` target, distinct hook. New packages: `@capacitor/share` + `@capacitor/filesystem` (both v8). Tenure caption discipline: zero `member`/`subscriber`/`anniversary`/`milestone` strings in the codebase (grep-verified). **Deferred to follow-up:** 1200×630 social aspect, multi-week carousel, custom themes, auto-share-on-Sunday — story aspect + manual button only at v1. Contract: `docs/contracts/api/og-weekly-zone-card.md`. |
| One free Kit insight per week (KIT-TASTE-01) | FREE (Haiku-backed) | 2026-05-23 | Cialdini-reciprocity + Eyal variable-reward partial-release conversion lever — free users get one Haiku-generated weekly insight on the Coach tab, sourced entirely from manual RPE + fatigue tags (Strava-independent). New route `app/api/coaching/weekly-free-insight/route.ts` is pull-on-view (no cron); free-tier-only — paid/trial return 400 because they have the full weekly report. Migration `20260525_free_insights.sql` adds `free_insights (user_id, week_start_date, headline, body, ai_model, created_at)` with composite PK + RLS; ISO-Monday-week cache key in the user's local timezone means one Haiku call per user per week. Route returns a discriminated `state` ∈ {`insight`, `risk_gated`, `insufficient`, `unavailable`} so the Coach tab renders deterministic UI for every case. `CoachTeaser` rewritten in `app/dashboard/DashboardClient.tsx` to fetch on mount and render four branches: insight card (`CoachByline` + AIMark + headline + body), amber rule-engine warning (no AIMark — output is not from the model), insufficient-logs hint, and the original dimmed fallback. Risk gate reuses `lib/coaching/reframeRiskGate.ts` (HR drift fields passed null on free tier); the gate fires on `coaching_flag` accumulation and consecutive Heavy/Wrecked fatigue tags, silencing the AI in favour of a rule-engine line. Prompt builder `lib/coaching/prompts/freeInsight.ts` composes via `buildVoiceHeader`; output is JSON parsed by the route with cheerleader-word backstop. Eligibility: ≥2 `session_completions` rows with `status='complete' AND rpe IS NOT NULL` in the last 7 days — fewer than that returns the empty-state. Only AI route that fires for free users; every other AI surface remains paid/trial-only. Contract: `docs/contracts/api/weekly-free-insight.md`. |

Title `BRAND.push.trialInsight = "Kit noticed something."`; body is the first sentence of the latest `run_analysis.feedback_text` truncated at 100 chars on a word boundary (`app/api/push/send-trial-insight/route.ts → firstSentence`). Migration `20260524_trial_insight_push.sql` adds `trial_insight_push_sent_at TIMESTAMPTZ NULL` on `user_settings`; stamp is never cleared (one-shot for life). Strava-independent — `run_analysis` is populated by either Strava or HealthKit pipelines. Cron auth accepts both Vercel's `Authorization: Bearer <CRON_SECRET>` and the legacy `x-cron-secret` header; both GET and POST handlers route to the same logic. Out of scope: localised time-of-day delivery (lands when it lands in the 4–6 hour window), additional trial-day nudges. Contract: `docs/contracts/api/push-send-trial-insight.md`. |

Title is composed by `lib/coaching/voiceLines.ts → buildDailyPushTitle` from `BRAND.push.dailyTraining` + session type + concise metric (e.g. `"Today: easy 45m."`); body is the per-type voice line via `getSessionVoiceLine`. Skip conditions: free tier, opted-out, no plan, rest/cross-train/strength day, already sent today, Today screen opened in last 30 min. The 30-min suppression depends on a new `POST /api/me/today-heartbeat` route that DashboardClient fires fire-and-forget whenever the active screen becomes `today`. Strava-independent — source is plan + session catalogue, no run data on this path. | Strava per-km splits + pace-fade signal (AI-DEPTH-02b) | PAID | 2026-05-24 | Closes the per-km splits half of AI-DEPTH-02 (HR drift / AI-DEPTH-02a shipped 2026-05-11; HealthKit-side bucketing still pending under AI-DEPTH-02c). Migration `20260527_strava_activities_splits_metric.sql` adds `splits_metric JSONB` to `strava_activities`. Both Strava ingest paths persist `activity.splits_metric` on write: `app/api/webhooks/strava/route.ts` + `app/api/strava/link-activity/route.ts`. New pure module `lib/coaching/paceAnalysis.ts → computePaceFadeSummary()` consumes the splits array and returns `{firstHalfAvgPaceSecPerKm, backHalfAvgPaceSecPerKm, paceFadeSecPerKm, paceFadePct, splitsUsed, sparse}`. Guards: returns null when fewer than 6 usable full-km splits; excludes short-tail final splits < 600m (their pace is a watch-stop artefact, not a coaching signal); time-weights pace within each half so a stop-and-go split contributes proportionally to its length. Wired into both prompt builders (`lib/coaching/prompts/sessionFeedback.ts` + `sessionReframe.ts`) as a `paceFadeBlock` companion to the existing HR-drift `streamBlock`. Reference rule the prompt enforces: ≥15 s/km back-half fade triggers direct citation; cross-reference with HR drift to distinguish aerobic (engine pushed) from muscular (legs went) limiter; negative split called out as a strength when even effort was prescribed. Also feeds the new limiter classifier (`lib/coaching/limiter.ts`) which gains a `muscular` category specifically for pace fade with stable HR — closing the loop between deterministic signal and AI surface. Fetched in `app/api/post-run-reframe/route.ts` alongside `avg_temp_c` in a single supabase query keyed on whichever source ref (`strava_activity_id` or `apple_health_uuid`) the completion carries. 9 unit tests in `paceAnalysis.test.ts`. HealthKit-side bucketing (the original 02b sub-scope's other half) remains open as AI-DEPTH-02c — once that lands, HK-sourced runs get the same pace-fade signal with no consumer changes. |
| AI prompt enrichments — limiter, temperature, observational phrasing (AI-ENRICH-01) | PAID | 2026-05-24 | Three sibling additions to the per-session AI surfaces, scoped mid-flight from a ChatGPT-prompt elite-coach analysis (the 9-section essay format was rejected as wrong-product; the depth ideas were transplanted). All three apply to `lib/coaching/prompts/sessionFeedback.ts` + `sessionReframe.ts`. **(1) Limiter hypothesis classifier.** New `lib/coaching/limiter.ts → inferLimiter()` — deterministic rule engine returning `{category, confidence, reasoning} \| null` across 7 categories: `heat`, `recovery`, `aerobic` (HR drifted, pace held), `muscular` (pace faded, HR held — depends on AI-DEPTH-02b), `pacing`, `execution`, `fueling`, `fatigue_tag`. Source-tiered: full-data path uses HR + stream + pace fade + temp + RPE; manual loggers fall through to the `fatigue_tag` branch from RPE + Heavy/Wrecked accumulation. Thresholds in `constants.ts → LIMITER` per Configuration Singularity doctrine. Surfaced only at medium/high confidence — low-confidence guesses suppressed (would undermine Kit's authority). Prompts instruct "high = fact, medium = hypothesis" framing. 19 unit tests in `limiter.test.ts`. Inputs threaded through `app/api/analyse-run/route.ts` + `app/api/post-run-reframe/route.ts` (the latter reuses the risk-gate fatigue array to avoid a redundant query). **(2) Temperature ingest.** Migration `20260527_strava_activities_avg_temp.sql` adds `avg_temp_c NUMERIC(5,2)` to `strava_activities`. Both Strava ingest paths (`webhooks/strava/route.ts` + `strava/link-activity/route.ts`) persist `activity.average_temp` on write. Prompts surface temperature only when ≥22°C or ≤4°C with explicit heat/cold instructions: hot days suppress HR-discipline lectures ("name the heat, don't lecture discipline" — HR 10bpm above zone in 28°C is execution noise, not failure), cold days warn about first-km warmup creep. Null on HealthKit / manual / pre-migration Strava — prompt block silently omits. **(3) Observational phrasing bias.** Single line added to `lib/coaching/prompts/voiceRules.ts → buildVoiceHeader` biasing Kit toward "This suggests…", "The limiter looks like…", "A key distinction is…" stems. Applies to every coaching surface — pushes Kit away from dashboard register toward coach interpretation. **Why bundled here, not in AI-DEPTH-02b:** all three shipped in the same commit but represent distinct work threads — AI-DEPTH-02b was the pre-tracked backlog item; these three were scoped mid-flight from the same elite-coach prompt analysis. Not in backlog — emergent / scoped mid-flight. |

Arc-fill (not stroke-thickness) chosen so the brand mark's silhouette stays intact at any data shape — only the *coverage* of each ring varies. Other surfaces (login, OG cards, marketing) keep the static mark. Localised functional use, not a full takeover — documented in the Pattern 25 entry. **ui-patterns.md:** §11 RestraintCard's "permanent slot on Today" doctrine marked superseded with a status note; §25 ZoneRings and §26 Voice Anchor Strip added with full anatomy, props, and tradeoff disclosure. Personalised from the latest of the user's last 10 `run_analysis` rows (`RunSummary`: load km, HR-in-zone %, verdict, analysed-run count, weekday) — **board-mandated voice: surface a specific run + what it showed, never scarcity**. First name from `user_metadata.full_name`. Sender `kit@zonna.run`. Templates: `lib/email/trialEmailTemplates.ts → buildDay11Email/buildDay14Email`; transport: `lib/email/resend.ts → sendEmail`. Migration `20260608_trial_email_stamps.sql` (two nullable TIMESTAMPTZ stamps on `user_settings`). Commit `7cde428`. Requires repo secrets `CRON_SECRET` + `VERCEL_URL` in GitHub Actions (same pattern as HOOK-SCHED-01). **Delivery confirmed live 2026-06-22** — founder observed trial emails arriving, so secrets are set and the daily cron is firing green. |

| Long-run shortfall detection (ENGINE-02) | PAID | 2026-06-22 | Catches the silent pattern of long runs logged "complete" but consistently cut short. Fires when the `LONG_RUN_SHORTFALL_CONSECUTIVE` (2) most-recent **long** runs each finish under `LONG_RUN_SHORTFALL_COMPLETION_PCT` (82%) of planned distance (≈ `distance_score ≤ 50`), in adjacent weeks. Response: a `reduce_volume` adjustment trimming the upcoming long run by `LONG_RUN_SHORTFALL_REDUCE_PCT` (15%) with a matter-of-fact coach note; `requiresConfirmation: true` (one-tap confirm — structural change to a key session). Distinct from `fatigue_accumulation` (watches fatigue tags) and `skip_with_reason` (explicit user skips). Easy-run shortfalls are out of scope (§1 — zone discipline > volume discipline → coach note only). Peak long run protected by the shared `guardCheck` taper guards (no automatic trigger inside `TAPER_PROTECTION_WEEKS` or during taper). **Most of the engine landed in `b605088` alongside ENGINE-01 but was never finalised; the 2026-06-22 ship adds the missing CHECK-constraint migration `20260622_engine_trigger_types.sql` (without which the insert failed), CoachingPrinciples §66, the `long_run_shortfall` AI few-shot, and unit tests.** Engine: `buildLongRunShortfallAdjustment` in `lib/coaching/planAdjustment.ts`. Route: `app/api/adjust-plan/route.ts` (builds `recentLongRunAnalyses`). Constants: `LONG_RUN_SHORTFALL_*` in `lib/coaching/constants.ts`. Tests: `lib/coaching/planAdjustment.test.ts` (5 cases). User-facing disclosure: "What we watch for" in MeScreen already names it. ⚠️ Migration must be applied to the live Supabase DB for the trigger to persist. |

A **fourth readiness sub-signal** `isPoorSleepQuality` (`lib/coaching/readinessBaseline.ts → computeReadiness`) fires when total sleep was adequate (≥ `SLEEP_THRESHOLD_HOURS`) but deep sleep was below `DEEP_SLEEP_PCT_FLOOR` (10%) of staged sleep — folded into the existing OR-composite in `checkAdjustmentTriggers` and `buildReadinessAdjustment` (reason "deep sleep low") in `lib/coaching/planAdjustment.ts`, surfaced by `/api/pre-session-readiness`. Deliberately the weakest signal: conservative floor, never fires on short nights (that's `isShortSleep`), never fires without a real stage breakdown (undifferentiated "asleep" → no signal, no false positive). Config: `GENERATION_CONFIG.READINESS.DEEP_SLEEP_PCT_FLOOR`. Principle: CoachingPrinciples §59 (extended). Tests: `lib/coaching/readinessBaseline.test.ts` (5 cases). ⚠️ Migration must be applied to the live Supabase DB; real stage data flow needs on-device verification. |

| Password reset / forgot-password (AUTH-RESET-01) | FREE (infra) | 2026-06-22 | Closes a real gap: email/password signups had no recovery path (OAuth users were unaffected). **Request:** "Forgot password?" link in signin mode on `app/auth/login/page.tsx` swaps the card to a focused reset-request view (email → `supabase.auth.resetPasswordForEmail(email, { redirectTo: '<origin>/auth/reset' })`); neutral "if that email has an account…" message never reveals whether the address exists (Supabase returns success for unknown emails). **Landing:** new `app/auth/reset/page.tsx` — handles the recovery link two ways so it's robust regardless of email-template config: **(1) `token_hash` + `type=recovery` → `verifyOtp` (PREFERRED — no PKCE `code_verifier` needed, works cross-device and on iOS-native where the email opens in Safari, not the Capacitor webview); (2) `?code=` → browser-client auto-exchange via `detectSessionInUrl` (same-device PKCE fallback).** 5s timeout → "Link expired" state. Then `updateUser({ password })` (min 8 + confirm-match) → hard-nav `/dashboard`. State machine: verifying / ready / invalid / saving / done; matches the login aesthetic (Wordmark, Warm Slate, `TextField`). Middleware allows the unauthenticated landing (session is only established client-side here). **⚠️ One-time Supabase dashboard setup for cross-device/native to work:** set the *Reset Password* email template link to `{{ .SiteURL }}/auth/reset?token_hash={{ .TokenHash }}&type=recovery`, and add `/auth/reset` to Auth → URL Configuration → Redirect URLs. Without it, only the same-device `?code=` fallback works. Verified in preview: login link/toggle, real Supabase request (neutral message, no console errors), reset no-token "Link expired". The `verifyOtp`→set-password path needs a real recovery email to exercise end-to-end. No migration. |

(no CTA, real-world action); **no-hr** (runs exist, RHR or MaxHR missing) → "One more thing." + "Set heart rate →" CTA (routes to Benchmark); **last-week** (stale weekly report) → "Generate report" stays. CTA button replaces "Generate report" in the empty states. Auto-resolves on next render. Recalibrates `weekly_km` + all session `distance_km` proportionally; session types, quality counts, rest days, and race week are unchanged. Idempotent via `plan.meta.taper_recalibrated_at`. Pure rule engine: `lib/plan/taperRecalibration.ts → computeTaperRecalibration()`. API route: `POST /api/recalibrate-taper` (gated on `dynamic_reshape_r20`). Wired into `DashboardClient` at the paid coaching data load on taper entry. All thresholds in `GENERATION_CONFIG.TAPER_RECAL_*`. Principle: CoachingPrinciples §68. | Day-boundary doctrine §65 + Coach "X behind" fix | FREE | 2026-06-19 | Traynor flagged the pattern after the weekly-report fix earlier the same day: "this is the second 'in-flight vs done' bug we've shipped." Today is in flight until midnight — every surface comparing "done" vs "planned" must treat today's not-yet-done session as in flight, not missed. Shared helper `lib/coaching/dayBoundary.ts → daysDueByEndOfYesterday(weekStart, now)` returns the strictly-past day-keys; no upper cap so stale calls after week end naturally return all 7. Audit of three suspects: `/api/push/send-daily` clean (morning-only forward-looking push), `acuteChronicRatio` + `disciplineLedger` clean (action signals, not date arithmetic), Coach `sessionsContext` BUG — verdict label read "2 behind" at noon Wednesday when no day was actually missed. Fixed: new `liveSessionsDueToDate` prop on `CoachScreen` computed via the helper; `sessionsContext` now takes `dueToDate` as third arg and judges behind/on-track against due-by-yesterday. Headline number unchanged ("X / full-week-planned" stays honest, the runner can see what's still ahead). "Complete" still requires `done >= planned` (end-of-week verdict). Weekly-report route refactored to call the same helper — single source of truth, future "in-flight" bug can't ship a third time. 9 regression tests in `lib/coaching/dayBoundary.test.ts` lock the exact off-by-one. CoachingPrinciples §65 documents the principle, the audit, the wire-in. Commit `dbb23f5`. |
| HR sync latency absorption — backend (HR-SYNC-01) | PAID (infra) | 2026-06-24 | Apple Watch HR samples sync to HealthKit on Apple's schedule, often hours after the workout shell — pre-fix, 2/3 founder runs had HR null permanently because rows aged out of the retry window. Layer 1 backend half of the ADR-011 §5 absorption strategy. **Pure helpers**: `lib/coaching/hrPending.ts → classifyHrPending(row, now)` (returns `has-hr` / `pending` / `fallback` / `not-applicable`) + `lib/coaching/lateArrivalGate.ts → decideLateArrival(input, now)` (shared 24h boundary). **Late-arrival gate (Hutchinson)**: HR arriving > 24h after workout end patches the columns + stamps `hr_arrived_late_at` but does NOT fire a fresh `/api/analyse-run` — no two-day-stale post-run reframe. Fresh arrivals (≤ 24h) re-trigger analyse-run via shared `triggerHrRefreshAnalysis` helper. Applied to both ingest paths: same-uuid re-sync ("Fix A") in `/api/health/ingest` AND cross-source consolidate patch in `lib/coaching/healthkitConsolidate.ts → consolidateIncomingHealthKitRow` (the latter is new behaviour — previously the patch was orphaned from coaching). **Foreground retry sweep**: `lib/health/clientSync.ts → retryPendingHrRows()` added to `syncOnAppOpen()`. Every app foreground scans HR-null `source='apple_health'` rows from the last 48h, re-queries `Health.readSamples({ dataType: 'heartRate', startDate, endDate })`, and re-posts to `/api/health/ingest` if samples now exist. Patches via the existing consolidate helper. **UI retry wrapper**: `retryHrFromUi()` exported — same path with a 30s throttle so a tap on the fallback-row button can't hammer HealthKit. **Migration `20260624_hr_sync_instrumentation.sql`** (applied to live Supabase 2026-06-24): `hr_present_at_first_query: boolean` (set ONCE on insert, preserved on resync — drives the "% of runs with HR at first query" cohort metric); `hr_arrived_late_at: timestamptz` + partial index (drives the > 24h-late carry-across for tomorrow's daily coach note). Tests: `hrPending.test.ts` (9 cases pinning the 24h boundary + clock-skew), `lateArrivalGate.test.ts` (6 cases including the founder Row-3 case), extended `healthkitConsolidate.test.ts` (3 INV-DATA-008 sentinels). Commit `77824b8`. |
| HR sync latency absorption — Swift observer bridge (HR-SYNC-03) | PAID (infra) | 2026-06-24 | Layer 2 of ADR-011 §5. Custom Capacitor 8 plugin `HealthObserverPlugin` (`ios/App/App/HealthObserverPlugin.swift/.m`) registers `HKObserverQuery` + `enableBackgroundDelivery(.immediate)` for `heartRate`, `restingHeartRate`, `heartRateVariabilitySDNN`, `sleepAnalysis`. When Apple Watch syncs new samples, iOS notifies the app (foreground or warm-background); observer calls `notifyListeners` → TS binding `lib/health/observer.ts → initHealthObserver()` → `syncOnAppOpen()` → pending HR resolves before the user re-opens the app. Wired into `CapacitorBoot.tsx`. No new entitlements (existing `com.apple.developer.healthkit` covers background delivery). Graceful degradation: fully-killed app picks up HR on next cold-start foreground sync. `packageClassList` in `capacitor.config.json` must include `"HealthObserverPlugin"` — re-add after every `cap sync`. Commit `e341808`. |
| HR sync latency absorption — UX (HR-SYNC-02) | PAID (infra) | 2026-06-24 | Layer 1 UX half. Honest brand absorption of HealthKit sync latency — the SLT review 2026-06-24 endorsed the position that surfacing a half-coached card (no HR, no zone discipline) violates the "Hold the zone" promise. Better to name the wait. **New primitives**: `components/shared/HrPendingStatusRow.tsx` (pulsing moss dot + status text, two sizes, in-flight "Checking…" state, tappable button when state='fallback') and `components/shared/PendingHrCard.tsx` (front-of-morph-chain card, mirrors PendingAnalysisCard structure for a sub-perceptual cross-fade). **No CoachByline / AIMark on either** — rule-engine state, not AI provenance (Pattern 16 § "When NOT to use"). Eyebrow label `HEART RATE` + pulsing dot replaces the byline. **SessionCard extended** with three new props (`hrPendingState`, `onHrRetry`, `isHrRetrying`). When set on a done card: renders the status row beneath the provenance line (TODAY-ATTR-01 coexists), suppresses the muted/transparent done-state treatment (run still matters), bumps min-height 64→76px. **PostRunScreen morph chain extended** to three states (PendingHrCard → PendingAnalysisCard → RunFeedbackCard) — pending state suppresses zone discipline, risk gate, reframe, AI coach note. Loads HR-pending classification fields via a targeted strava_activities select on mount when the completion is HK-linked. **TodayScreen wiring**: SessionCard render computes pending state inline by looking up the matching activity in `stravaRuns` by `apple_health_uuid`. Local retry state (`retryingForHrUuid`) gates the "Checking…" display per card. **Copy** (Sutherland-tightened, no time promise, responsibility on the device): *"Watch HR hasn't landed yet."* (pending) / *"HR didn't land. Tap to retry."* (fallback) / *"Checking…"* (in-flight). Verb mirror across states is intentional. No notification on pending-resolve (Wood — the post-run app-open is the trigger; resolution is found, not announced). **Animation**: reuses existing `ai-mark-pulse` keyframe with wrapper opacity 0.7 (quietens the 0.55→1 range to 0.385→0.7 so it reads as system state, not AI). PendingHrCard fades in via `zonna-fade-in` 200ms. **Doctrine**: INV-DATA-005 (absent metric must explain why — the pending state IS the explanation), INV-DATA-008 (HK-SOR, no Strava reach). Commit `77824b8`. |
| Reshape engine correctness fixes (RESHAPE-FIX-WAVE1) | PAID (infra) | 2026-06-26 | Three correctness fixes on the `dynamic_reshape_r20` path surfaced by a live taper-week incident; no behaviour change, no rule change. (1) **Silent save failure (Defect 9)** — `lib/plan.ts → savePlanForUser` previously awaited the Supabase upsert without checking `.error`, so RLS rejections, constraint failures, and schema issues vanished. Live data showed the founder's `plans.updated_at` had been stuck for 9 days while the engine kept writing `auto_applied` rows that never persisted. Now both the upsert AND the `plan_weekly_notes` invalidation throw on `.error` — better a visible 500 than a phantom success that corrupts downstream coaching reads. (2) **Day-index drift (Defect 1)** — `app/api/adjust-plan/route.ts:206` used `Object.values(week.sessions).filter(Boolean)` which returns values in object-key insertion order. The founder's live `plan_json` had week-23 session keys stored in `fri, mon, sat, sun, thu, tue, wed` order; the engine read Sunday's long run as "Thursday's session" because Sun's value happened to sit at array index 3. Replaced with explicit `DAY_ORDER.map(d => week.sessions[d] ?? null)` so the array is guaranteed 7 entries indexed mon=0 … sun=6. (3) **Make-up session dropped (Defect 2)** — `lib/coaching/planAdjustment.ts:516` did `[...sessionsAfter, makeUpSession]` which appended at array index 7; the apply loop only reads indices 0–6, so every `skip_with_reason` make-up was silently discarded on confirm. Fixed: write the make-up INTO the `freeDay` slot (replacing the rest day the builder already identified as the landing site). **New defensive invariant** in `checkAdjustmentTriggers` entry point — throws if `currentWeekSessions.length !== 7` or any slot is null/non-object. The throw surfaces to the route's 500; this class of bug is now loud, not silent. Knock-on caller fix in `DashboardClient.tsx:1933` — the `void savePlanForUser(...)` fire-and-forget for plan.meta HR sync now `.catch`es the throw rather than producing an unhandled rejection. 3 new regression tests in `lib/coaching/planAdjustment.test.ts`. Docs: `plan-invariants.md` documents the new reshape-time invariant; `backlog.md` opens Wave 2a/2b/2c/3 + OPS-01 monitoring. No migration. SLT review 2026-06-26 authorised. |
| Calibrated reshape authority — magnitude rule (RESHAPE-FIX-WAVE3 Phase 1) | PAID | 2026-06-26 | Replaces the original SLT "kill auto-apply" framing per Wood's calibrated-threshold synthesis. Single decision point: `lib/coaching/reshapeMagnitude.ts → computeReshapeMagnitude(proposed)` returns `'high' | 'low'`. Always-high triggers (skip_with_reason, session_reorder, readiness_signal); always-low (`adjustmentType === 'flag_for_review'`); otherwise structural-diff inspection — `replaced`/`added`/`removed` → high, `modified` > 15% per session → high, week-total > 15% → high. `/api/adjust-plan` calls the helper once after the builder runs and uses the verdict as the authoritative `requiresConfirmation`; builder-level flags no longer consulted (kept on `ProposedAdjustment` for informational provenance only). Manual triggers still force pending. **The 2026-06-26 incident class is now structurally impossible** — `session_reorder` is unconditionally high-magnitude regardless of whether §7 alternation fires, so the tue↔thu swap that landed the long run on Tuesday would have surfaced a confirmation tile with the Wave 2A diff. **New `GENERATION_CONFIG.RESHAPE_AUTOAPPLY_THRESHOLDS`** block holds the 15% / 15% defaults — mirrors the existing `LOAD_RATIO.watch` 15% trim so the engine's standard reduce-volume stays sub-threshold by design (habit-formation automaticity preserved, per Wood). **New ADR-012** documents the architectural rationale + the three SLT positions considered. **New CoachingPrinciples §69** anchors the magnitude principle in coaching language; the line table maps trigger types and diff signals to magnitude. 9 new unit tests in `reshapeMagnitude.test.ts` including the 2026-06-26 incident shape as the lead regression case. **Phase 2** (Me-screen audit surface, NOTIF-01 quietness rules, `validatePlan()` reshape integration) tracked as `RESHAPE-FIX-WAVE3-PHASE2` in backlog. 238 total tests pass; typecheck clean. No migration. SLT 2026-06-26 authorised. |
| Bare-stub completion gate — write path (RESHAPE-FIX-WAVE2B) | PAID | 2026-06-26 | Closes Defect 10 from the 2026-06-26 reshape audit. The incident's phantom completion (session_day=sun, status=complete, every other field null) was written by tapping "Confirm complete" on the Strava-log view with no activity selected — a path that previously fired `saveCompletion('complete')` with no metadata, producing a row the engine treated as a verified done session. Same path also fired from non-run "Mark as done" buttons. **Scope decision during build**: instead of a `verified` column migration affecting all 25 `session_completions` consumer callsites in one PR, gate at the WRITE boundary so future completions cannot be bare stubs, and ship a pure helper for downstream READ filtering. The 6-surface read audit ships as `RESHAPE-FIX-WAVE2B-AUDIT` in backlog — independently reviewable per surface. **Three button-path changes** in `DashboardClient.tsx`: (1) line ~3526 `handleMarkComplete` non-run branch — `void saveCompletion('complete')` → `setView('reflect')`; (2) line ~4520 non-run "Mark as done" button — `saveCompletion('complete')` → `setView('reflect')`; (3) line ~4579 Strava-log view "Confirm complete" — conditional: with `selectedActivity` fires the existing path; without, routes to reflect and the button label becomes "Log without activity." All three now require at least one RPE/fatigue chip-tap on the reflect view before the row is created via the existing `saveReflect` upsert. Strava-linked auto-match path unchanged. **New helper** `lib/coaching/completionVerification.ts` — `isVerifiedCompletion(c)` returns true if the row has an activity link OR rpe OR fatigue_tag; `isBareStubCompletion(c)` is the read-side mirror for analytics filtering. 11 unit tests including the exact 2026-06-26 phantom shape as the lead regression case. **ADR-011 §3 amended** with new § 3b "Verified-Completion Rule" — RPE-as-minimum doctrine codified, write/read boundary roles documented, historical bare stubs noted as un-migrated and read-boundary-filtered. **No `session_completions` schema change.** 229 total tests pass; typecheck clean. SLT 2026-06-26 authorised. |
| AI summary fidelity on plan adjustments (RESHAPE-FIX-WAVE2A) | PAID | 2026-06-26 | Closes Defect 5 from the 2026-06-26 reshape audit. The AI summary on Row 2 of the incident said *"the 24km run and hard-easy rhythm stay intact"* while the engine had just moved the long run sun → tue. Anti-confabulation rules in the prompt failed. Two-layer fix per SLT (Wood + Hutchinson): prose handles WHY (AI, with CoachByline + AIMark), rule-engine diff handles WHAT (no AIMark — mixing provenance destroys the signal). **Pure diff utility** `lib/coaching/diff/sessionDiff.ts` exports `computeSessionDiff`, `summariseDiff`, `labelSession` — `replaced` is the structural case (different type), `modified` is the trim case (same type, different size). **AdjustmentDiff component** `components/shared/AdjustmentDiff.tsx` renders per-day before → after rows under the prose; strikethrough on before, accent on after; auto-hides when no day changed (so coaching-note-only adjustments stay prose-only). **Prompt rewrite** `lib/coaching/prompts/planAdjustment.ts` now feeds the model the explicit structural diff and adds two hard rules: #5 forbids enumerating the diff in prose ("the diff component shows that — you write the WHY"); #6 forbids stability claims about anything in the diff ("X stays intact" is reserved for sessions NOT in the diff). **Runtime validator** `lib/coaching/diff/validateAiSummary.ts` rejects AI output that contradicts the diff via four patterns: type stability + changed type, day stability + changed day, distance stability + changed distance (catches the exact incident: "24km run stays" without naming "long"), invented moves where neither day changed. Reject → fall back to rule-engine summary silently (ADR-006 hybrid generation pattern). Console.warn for telemetry on rejection so prompt drift surfaces. **PendingAdjustmentBanner extended** with optional `sessionsBefore`/`sessionsAfter` props; both consumers in `DashboardClient.tsx` (`AdjustmentBanner` wrapper + `ReshapeScreen`) pass them through. 15 new tests across `sessionDiff.test.ts` (8) + `validateAiSummary.test.ts` (7), including the exact 2026-06-26 incident summary as the lead regression case ("REJECTS the exact 2026-06-26 incident summary"). 218 total tests pass; typecheck clean. **No migration** — `plan_adjustments` already stores `sessions_before` + `sessions_after`; client computes diff at render. Docs: `brand.md` § Adjustment Voice — Two-Layer Honesty; `ui-patterns.md` Pattern 10 rewrite. SLT 2026-06-26 authorised. |
| Move-confirmation step on PlanCalendar (RESHAPE-FIX-WAVE2C) | PAID | 2026-06-26 | Closes Defect 11 from the 2026-06-26 reshape audit: the move icon on `PlanCalendar` was a 3-line hamburger at 0.45 opacity that read as "more options," and tapping it then tapping a target day immediately wrote to `session_overrides` + posted `/api/adjust-plan` — no preview, no confirm. The incident runner reported "I never dragged it!" — true; he'd tapped without realising it initiated a structural move. *Build note: rescoped during implementation from the SLT-approved "kill tap-to-move, drag works" framing because no HTML5 drag exists in `PlanCalendar.tsx` (zero `onDrag`/`onDrop`/`draggable` handlers); killing tap-to-move would have left users with no move capability at all.* Three changes: (1) New `pendingMove` state in `WeekCard` stages the move between target-tap and the actual write. (2) Inline confirmation row (Pattern 10b — see `ui-patterns.md`) renders below the day rows with concrete copy — "Move Long run from Sun to Thu?" with a "X swaps to Y" call-out when applicable. Confirm fires the existing `onMove`/`onSwap` callbacks; Cancel clears state with zero DB side-effect. No popup — Pattern 30/§ "No popups" rule. (3) Move-icon affordance bumped from hamburger to a moss-soft `↕ Move` pill with `aria-label` — explicit glyph + label removes the ambiguity. Typecheck clean. No migration. No test additions (no component-test infrastructure in this repo today; behaviour change is UI-only and verified by manual smoke). Docs: `ui-patterns.md` Pattern 10b documents the row + affordance; `backlog.md` ✅ entry records the rescope rationale. Originally framed as Wave 2c of the RESHAPE-FIX programme. |
| Race-aware debrief across every coaching surface + ultra first-class (RACE-DEBRIEF 01–03) | PAID | 2026-07-14 | Extends §70's weekly-report race-debrief to the Today note, the run-analysis feedback, and the post-run reframe, and makes ultra distance first-class on the debrief surfaces. Originating incident: a founder finished a 100km ultra (injured at 60k, backed off early for heat) and got a deflating Today debrief ("race didn't land where you wanted … not a referendum") + a mis-attributing run analysis ("faded across the back third / running out of gas … needed to start conservatively — which you did"). **Phase 1 (§71):** `lib/coaching/prompts/dailyCoachNote.ts` suppresses the plan-scoring `verdict` for a race (both branches) and leads the plan-complete debrief with the deterministic `achievementLine` (CA-03, reused) — forbids shortfall/"didn't land" language; `lib/coaching/limiter.ts → inferLimiter()` returns `null` for a race, an ultra-distance effort (`LIMITER.SUPPRESS_ULTRA_DISTANCE_KM = 50`, INV-CFG-001), or an athlete-reported injury (`injuryFlagged`); `sessionFeedback.ts` + `sessionReframe.ts` drop the zone/HR-drift/pace-fade citations for a race and swap in race-debrief framing. **Phase 2:** new pure module `lib/coaching/raceNarrative.ts` (`buildRaceNarrativeBlock` + `raceInjuryFlagged` + `RACE_INJURY_KEYWORDS`) threads `Week.result_embedded` (the runner's own authoritative account — outcome/notes/what_broke) into `sessionFeedback` + `sessionReframe` via `analyse-run` + `post-run-reframe` routes; `injuryFlagged` feeds the limiter guard; temperature un-gated on the race path with ADR-011 §5 honest-absence (never fabricated, absent for HK-only); daily-note `achievementLine` enriched with logged finish time + outcome. Daily note gets the result via `achievementLine` only (not raw narrative) — stays a witness/recovery line, not analysis. **Phase 3 (§72):** ultra was already first-class in plan generation (recovery buckets §62, intensity §1, ultra long-run §24e, peak floors §46, PLAN_SIGNATURES 50K/100K); the remaining gap was that `sessionFeedback`/`sessionReframe` gated fade-citations on race-*type*, so a non-race 50km+ long run still got a "start slower" lecture — now suppressed for any effort ≥ the shared ultra threshold, framed as time-on-feet (non-race ultra keeps its scored training read; only fade framing changes). Doctrine: CoachingPrinciples §71 + §72. Tests: `limiter.test.ts` (race/ultra/injury→null), `dailyCoachNote.test.ts` (verdict suppressed, achievement-led), `raceNarrative.test.ts`, first builder tests for `sessionFeedback` + `sessionReframe`. Route I/O contracts unchanged. Commits `701e376` (Phases 1+2) + `87ca30e` (Phase 3). 267 tests green; tsc + next build clean. Verified at prompt/logic level; live plan-complete-race browser journey still pending. |
| Post-race result prompt fires when race is the final plan week (POST-RACE-PROMPT-01) | PAID | 2026-07-14 | Third instance of the §65/§70 saturating-current-week-pointer bug class. `DashboardClient.tsx` gated the post-race "Race done. How did it go?" prompt (`postRaceState`) and the CA-03 goal ladder (`finishedRace`) on index comparisons (`currentWeekIndex > raceWeekIdx` / `currentWeekIndex <= idx`). `getCurrentWeekIndex()` saturates at the final week once today is past the plan, so both were unreachable when the goal race was in the last week (the normal shape) — the `RaceResultSheet` prompt never rendered, `Week.result_embedded` was never captured, and the whole downstream chain (CA-03 goal ladder, AI-DEPTH-08 post-race reshape, RACE-DEBRIEF-02 narrative enrichment) silently starved. Fix: new canonical `lib/plan.ts → isDatePastWeek(week, date)` (date ≥ end of the week's 7-day window), sibling to `isDateWithinWeek` / `isPlanComplete` (the latter refactored to delegate to it); both DashboardClient sites now use `isDatePastWeek(raceWeek, now)`. Doctrine: CoachingPrinciples §73 — "are we past plan-week N?" is a date-window question, never an index compare against the saturating current-week pointer. Tests: `planDateWindow.test.ts` (boundaries, final-week-race case, delegate parity). Commit `53e3890`. No migration. This fix activates the previously-dormant post-race surfaces for final-week-race users. |
| Race result persists on submit + surfaces immediately (POST-RACE-PROMPT-02) | PAID | 2026-07-14 | Follow-on to POST-RACE-PROMPT-01: the founder logged their 100k, submitted, and saw nothing. Three faults — (1) `RaceResultSheet.handleLogOnly` ("Log result only, keep my plan →") made no API call and silently discarded the result; (2) on the reshape path `result_embedded` only reached the live plan on **Accept**, so "Keep my plan as-is" threw the result away; (3) no success path updated client `plan` state, so the goal ladder/debrief didn't appear until reload, and a final-week race (no reshape) has no Accept button to find. Fix (CoachingPrinciples §74 — logging is an unconditional write, the reshape is an optional layer that never gates it, client reflects it immediately): `POST /api/post-race-reshape` always `savePlanForUser(embedRaceResult(plan))` before the reshape branch and returns the saved `plan`; new `offer_reshape` body flag persists the result without staging a pending reshape row; `RaceResultSheet` routes both buttons through one `submitResult(offerReshape)` and passes the saved plan up; `DashboardClient` `onLogOnly`/`onReshapeReady` call `setPlan(updatedPlan)` so the CA-03 "what's next" goal ladder appears as the acknowledgment (no popup — N-004). Sibling to the RESHAPE-FIX-WAVE2B write-boundary rule / ADR-011 §3b. Commit `0f3234f`. No migration; no route/component test infra in repo — verified via typecheck + build + natural device use. |

| Post-race maintenance block — "Base" (MAINT-01) | TIER-DIVERGENT | 2026-07-17 | Automatically appends maintenance weeks to `plan.weeks` when `isPlanComplete && result_embedded` (§73 + §74 — date-window + unconditional-write doctrine). Two phases: Phase 1 `maintenance_restoration` (easy/rest/cross-train only, volume follows `POST_RACE_RECOVERY_BY_DISTANCE` curve) and Phase 2 `maintenance_base` (easy + 1 quality/week from week 2, flat at 70% of plan peak). Duration is distance-keyed in `GENERATION_CONFIG.POST_RACE_MAINTENANCE_BLOCK`; RPE ≥ 8 extends Phase 1 by +1 week, DNF adds another +1 (stacking). Generator: `lib/plan/maintenance.ts → generateMaintenanceBlock()`. API: `POST /api/maintenance-block` (idempotent — second call is a no-op if maintenance weeks already exist). Trigger: `DashboardClient` `useEffect` fires once `finishedRace` is non-null and no maintenance-phase weeks exist; success calls `setPlan(updatedPlan)`. UI: dismissable "Base running while you decide what's next." card in `TodayScreen` (above CA-03 / NextGoalCard), keyed by race signature in `localStorage`. 5 new invariants in `validateMaintenanceBlock()` (`INV-MAINT-PHASE1-SESSION-TYPES`, `INV-MAINT-QUALITY-CAP`, `INV-MAINT-VOLUME-CEILING`, `INV-MAINT-REST-DAY`, `INV-MAINT-NO-RACE-SPECIFIC`). CoachingPrinciples §75 written. No new DB tables — maintenance weeks live in `plans.plan_json`. Plan structure + session display: FREE. AI coaching voice on sessions + weekly debrief notes: PAID (`maintenance_coaching` gate — not yet wired, silent fallback). 10 unit tests in `lib/plan/maintenance.test.ts`. Typecheck clean, 282/282 tests green. |
| Pull-to-refresh on the dashboard (PTR-01) | FREE | 2026-07-18 | Adds the conventional pull-down gesture to the single dashboard scroll container so all four primary screens (Today/Plan/Coach/Me) share one implementation. New shared component `components/shared/PullToRefresh.tsx` wraps the existing `scrollContainerRef` (passed in, so scroll-to-top on screen change still works) and is enabled only on primary nav screens + when `appReady`. On release it runs `DashboardClient.handleRefresh`: native `syncOnAppOpen()` (force HealthKit ingest) → `refreshHealthKitRuns()` + `refreshRunAnalysis()` + `refreshCompletions()` + `refreshUnreadNotifications()`. Honest use case = the post-run window (a just-finished run's verdict appears without backgrounding the app); daily-cached coaching (daily note, weekly report) is out of scope (server-cached per day). Indicator is a neutral `--moss` dot that **pulses** while refreshing (`zonna-ptr-pulse` keyframe in `globals.css`) — no spinner, and deliberately NOT the AIMark sparkle (provenance honesty: a data refresh isn't model output). Gesture engages only from `scrollTop<=0` on a vertical downward drag (axis-lock ignores week-strip horizontal swipes), `touchmove` bound non-passive to `preventDefault`. States: idle/pulling/armed/refreshing/done/error; respects `prefers-reduced-motion`. Completion is a two-line restraint beat — "Up to date." / "Nothing to chase." (held 1200ms) — pointing at release, never the next assignment (SLT board convergence: teaches restraint rather than manufacturing novelty, and disengages rather than re-engaging the over-trier). All modifiers stack; race-day RPE≥8/DNF still add weeks. `INV-MAINT-VOLUME-CEILING` re-based to base volume. Auto-live and editable, never accept/decline. Seen-flag stored on `result_embedded.maintenance_transition_seen` (self-keyed per race, no schema change, cross-device via `plan_json`); it precedes and suppresses the ongoing "Base running" status card. | Maintenance surfacing — corrections after live-data inspection (MAINT-05) | FREE | 2026-08-02 | A founder review of the live block showed MAINT-04's cadence fix missed the real cause (a subagent had hypothesised a recovery-jog leak without inspecting the plan; the plan had no recovery sessions). Now keyed on the first maintenance week in the current/future list, so it always renders; plus a screen-level "After the race · Maintenance" eyebrow under the plan title in `PlanScreen` when `currentWeek` is a maintenance week. **(3) Plan history shows the live plan** — `PlanHistoryScreen` now prepends the active plan (from `plans`, moss rail + "Current" tag, state-aware subtitle) so a single-plan user's current/just-finished plan is visible; `plan_archive` only ever held superseded plans, so it read as broken empty. 337 tests + typecheck + build green (4 new cadence tests). On race completion, `/api/maintenance-block` builds a maintenance-only plan (own `meta.plan_kind='maintenance'`, own name "After {race}", `race_date` cleared so race-only UI no-ops, `source_race_*` meta carried for copy + next-goal) and saves it as the sole active plan; **`savePlanForUser`'s existing race-change archive guard moves the completed race plan into `plan_archive`** (reused, not duplicated). So the finished race reads as completed in Plan History and maintenance is the only live plan — the founder's model. **No DB migration:** maintenance `week_n` stays CONTINUOUS (26+), and the app's week-number derivation was unified from array-position to canonical `week.n` (`PlanCalendar`, `TodayScreen`, `PlanScreen`, progress bar, weekly-note fetch) — a no-op on all current data (position already == `n`) that lets a standalone short-array maintenance plan key `session_completions`/`run_analysis` without colliding with the archived race plan's rows. Post-race surfaces (transition announcement, seen-flag, ongoing "Base running" card, CA-03 next-goal ladder) re-keyed off the archived race week onto the maintenance plan's `plan_kind`/`source_*` meta so they survive the handoff. Supersedes MAINT-04/05's appended-model seam + "Current"-race-row labelling. 337 tests + typecheck + build green. |

Every rule landed as `GENERATION_CONFIG` numeric + `CoachingPrinciples.md` section + invariant in the same commit (INV-CFG). **Governance added:** D-21 (a principle no plan can satisfy is a defect in the principle) + D-22 (changing canon routes through the board; honouring it does not). **Regression harness:** 7 golden-plan snapshots (P0 = User A's exact input) + property sweep gained an HR axis (414,720 plans). | Maintenance week-numbering split-brain fixed (MAINT-08) | FREE | 2026-08-08 | *Not in backlog — emergent, founder-reported during a live maintenance-plan review.* The Plan screen read **"Wk 29 of 11"** on the post-Race-to-the-Stones maintenance plan, and Plan History looked empty. Root cause: ADR-013 §27's "unify to `week.n`" was incomplete — the maintenance plan is the first case where array position (0–10) ≠ `week.n` (26–36), and `position == n` had masked every path before. **Display was over-applied** (`PlanScreen`→`PlanArc` and Today's header showed the `week.n` key, 29, instead of the array ordinal, 4) and **two routes were never converted** (`/api/weekly-report` keyed by position → read the archived race plan's rows; `/api/plan-weekly-note` did `plan.weeks[week_n-1]` → out of bounds → 404 → AI plan-voice card silently fell back to rule copy). `CoachScreen` keyed report-match/prev-week/phase-transition by the ordinal too. **Fix:** the two numbers are now named and separated everywhere — `weekKey` (canonical `week.n`, for every persisted week-scoped row) vs `weekOrdinal` (1-indexed array position, display + `PlanArc` only); new shared `lib/plan.ts → findWeekByN(weeks, n)` (resolves by `n`, never `weeks[n-1]`) used by both routes. Doctrine: **`week_n` is a key, `week.n` resolves it, array position is display only** (ADR-013 amendment 2026-08-08). Also made `savePlanForUser`'s archive guard **idempotent** (skip when an identical `race_name`+`race_date` snapshot was archived within a 10-min window — the handoff had archived RttS 3× via near-simultaneous saves) and cleaned up the 3 duplicate rows on the founder account. 8 new tests (`findWeekByN` ×4 + archive-guard ×4); 419 tests + typecheck + build green. |
| Post-race Phase 3 — the goal ladder waits for the re-engagement window (MAINT-07) | FREE (block + register) / PAID (CA-03 ladder, AI debrief) | 2026-08-02 | **Founder-reported defect → SLT decision.** On Today, the MAINT-04 maintenance announcement ("your body's still repairing — the plan's eased to base running") and the CA-03 goal ladder ("Run another 100K and take time off") rendered stacked, in the same viewport, giving contradictory advice at the highest-churn moment in the product. Root cause: §75's suppress-until-acknowledged rule was implemented against the ongoing "Base running" card only ([DashboardClient.tsx:1506](app/dashboard/DashboardClient.tsx:1506)); when MAINT-06 re-keyed the ladder onto the maintenance plan's `source_*` meta it began firing alongside the announcement. Board (Sutherland/Fried/Hutchinson/Wood/Traynor) rejected simple sequencing (option A) in favour of holding the ladder to **§75 Phase 3** — perceived readiness is least reliable exactly when the ladder asks for a racing decision (Hutchinson), and the ladder's dismissal persists for the whole block, so a day-one offer spends the single shot when the runner can least use it (Wood). **Phase 3 made first-class:** the final `PHASE3_LAST_WEEKS` (2) Phase 2 weeks — previously only a theme swap — now carry a `reengagement: true` marker. Deliberately NOT a third `phase` value: Phase 3 weeks are `maintenance_base` in every training respect (same volume, same quality cap, same invariants), so a new phase string would force ~14 `maintenance_restoration|maintenance_base` call sites to learn a case with no training meaning. **No migration:** `isReengagementWeek()` derives the window from the block's shape when the marker is absent, so plans generated before MAINT-07 reach Phase 3 untouched (verified against the live founder plan — derivation picked exactly the two weeks already carrying `PHASE3_THEME`). **Phase 3 arrives, not merely un-hides:** the ongoing card takes back the announcement's `--s-recov` rail and "After the race" eyebrow, so the block closes in the voice that opened it (ui-patterns Pattern 31); the PAID debrief keeps CoachByline + moss rail and suppresses the rule-engine marks — never two provenance signals on one card. Also fixed in passing: the card's hardcoded "Base running while you decide what's next" was forward-goal language during Phase 1, which §75 forbids — the card now renders the week's own (phase-correct) `theme`, making Phase 3's register shift free. **The wizard stays reachable throughout — this delays the proposal, never the action; no unlock or countdown copy anywhere.** Principles: §67 amended (ladder timing), §75 extended (Phase 3 surfacing). New invariant `INV-MAINT-REENGAGEMENT-WINDOW`. 7 new tests incl. the derived-fallback parity case; 344 tests + typecheck + build green; client bundle verified — generator and validator tree-shake out. |
| Display formatting, preference & date-aware plan singularity (ADR-015 / ADR-016) | TIER-DIVERGENT (infra — spans free cards/plan + paid push/coaching; nothing newly gated) | 2026-08-10 | *Not in backlog — emergent from the "78m push" investigation:* a daily push read "Easy 79m today" for a plan that hadn't started, in ambiguous units. Three singularities. **Format (ADR-015, `lib/format.ts`):** `formatDuration` is the sole owner of the ≥60→hours rule (`45 min` / `1h 18` / `2h`; never a bare "78m" — the minutes/miles/metres ambiguity that triggered this); `formatSessionMetric` the single distance-or-duration summary. Inline duration formatters in `voiceLines`/`SessionCard`/`PlanCalendar`/`sessionDiff`/`DashboardClient` (incl. session-structure parts) all migrated. **Temporal (ADR-016, `lib/plan.ts`):** `getSessionForDate` is the canonical "real session on calendar date D?" resolver (returns null before-start/after-end/gap/empty) + `isDateBeforePlan` (the missing before-start guard). `getCurrentWeek` saturated to week 1 before a plan began, so `send-daily`, `pre-session-readiness` and `weekly-report` now gate on the resolver — **the actual pre-start-push fix**. **Preference:** units + metric now honoured server-side (`lib/userPrefs.ts → getUserDisplayPrefs`/`normalizeDisplayPrefs` — no more hardcoded `'km'` on push/manual-feedback) and cross-device (per-session override moved localStorage → new **`session_metric_overrides`** table with one-time backfill, `lib/sessionMetricOverrides.ts`; migration `20260810_session_metric_overrides.sql`, applied live). New invariants INV-FMT-001/002, INV-PREF-001, INV-TIME-001 + a pre-ship checklist in the `zona-architectural-principles` skill. **Held follow-ons (in backlog W6):** FMT-01 (AI-prompt units — mi users still get km in LLM prose), FMT-02 (StravaPanel admin-only km), FMT-03 (delete dead `SessionHero`). Verified: tsc + 447 tests + production build. Commit `4314c43`. |
| Single-owner session classification (`sessionRole.ts`) + enrichment/shakeout/long-run fixes | TIER-DIVERGENT (infra — fixes span the FREE rule engine/invariants and PAID enrichment/coaching; nothing newly gated) | 2026-08-13 | *Not in backlog — emergent, discovered mid-flight during a coach-review PDF request:* one cluster of silent-failure bugs where session classification used the wrong signal, now consolidated under one owner `lib/plan/sessionRole.ts` (`isLongRun`/`isShakeout`/`coachingSessionType` + generator-stamped `Session.role`). **(1) Enrichment silently dead for ~4 months:** `z.record(DayKeySchema,…)` is exhaustive under Zod v4, so partial-week enricher output failed `EnrichedPlanSchema` → silent fallback to rule copy (ADR-006). Fixed with `z.partialRecord` (`schema.ts`). **(2) Shakeout invariant reverted enriched plans:** `INV-PLAN-MIN-SESSION-SIZE` exempted shakeouts by matching `"shakeout"` in the label, which the enricher rewrites → exemption lost → whole enriched plan reverted; now structural (race-week easy). **(3) `type === 'long'` was dead everywhere** (generator emits `type:'easy'`+role): long-run fatigue/readiness/shortfall trimming never fired, the §7 quality-long spacing invariant never ran, and readiness was gated off for long-run days — all migrated to `isLongRun`/`coachingSessionType` across `planAdjustment`, `pre-session-readiness`, `taperRecalibration`, `adjust-plan`, `analyse-run`, `post-run-reframe`, `weekly-free-insight`, `DashboardClient`, `BenchmarkUpdateScreen`. **Prevention:** pre-commit hook blocks label-based classification; **INV-CLASS-001…004** + `/ship` gate for new classifiers. **Residual (tracked):** enrichment failure is still only `recordOpsEvent`-visible — no surfaced probe/alert yet (pre-existing observability gap, not introduced here). Verified: tsc + 457 tests + property fuzzer 414,720 plans/0 violations + archetype 11/11 + production build. Commit `d5304ea`. |
| Reframe cohort — coaching-role matching (REFRAME-COHORT-01, `CoachingPrinciples §58` cut #2) | PAID (inherits `post_run_reframe` gate) | 2026-08-15 | First ruling of the Coaching Board (ADR-017) — **CORRECT WITH AMENDMENT**; the board found the backlog's "long-runs-only vs whole-easy-pool" framing was a false binary and that §58 already owned cohort matching, having deferred its own third axis to "cut #2". **Defect fixed (not a judgment call):** `recentRpePattern.sessionType` was always labelled `coachingSessionType()` ("long") while the pool was filtered on raw `session.type` — and since the generator models long runs as `type:'easy'` (INV-CLASS), the reframe printed *"your long runs"* over numbers drawn mostly from short easy runs (a claim about training the runner never did — the §61 failure). **Change:** both cohort sites in `app/api/post-run-reframe/route.ts` now filter via `coachingSessionType()` (canonical owner `lib/plan/sessionRole.ts` — no parallel classifier added); RPE window widened to 56d (`REFRAME_TIER.RPE_PATTERN_WINDOW_DAYS`) because narrowing alone left ~1 long run/week against a 4-sample floor in 28d, so one missed run killed the trend; completion ratio deliberately kept scoped to `TIER_B_WINDOW_DAYS` so an 8-week `completed` isn't divided by a 4-week `scheduled`; hardcoded `4` and `.limit(20)` promoted to config per INV-CFG-003. **Rationale for the role axis over §58's distance band alone** (Willy, corroborated by Sims): long-run RPE climbing 5→7 against flat short-easy 3s averages to no change when pooled, so a tissue-tolerance/LEA warning never fires. **Mechanical check is NOT `validatePlan()`** — that validates generated plans; this is a runtime read path. Locked by `lib/coaching/reframeCohort.test.ts` (8 cases incl. the label-must-match-pool invariant), recorded in `§60.7`. **Residuals (pre-existing, not introduced):** the tier-B block's `try/catch` degrades silently with only a `console.warn`; and legacy plans without a `role` stamp still classify by label, which the enricher may rewrite (governed by INV-CLASS; generator now stamps `role`). **Golden suite not re-run** — `buildSessionReframePrompt` is untouched, so the cases' fixed inputs→outputs are unaffected, but the suite is a manual AI-output comparison and is worth a pass before this reaches users. Verified: tsc clean + 465 tests. Commit `8e8e05a`. |
| Units in AI coaching prose (FMT-01) + dead `SessionHero` removal (FMT-03) | PAID (AI surfaces; FMT-03 is infra) | 2026-08-15 | A miles reader was getting **kilometres from every AI surface**. Distances and paces now render in the reader's unit across 11 prompt builders in `lib/coaching/prompts/*`, threaded from `getUserDisplayPrefs` in 9 routes (INV-PREF-001). **Backlog under-scoped it — two additions:** (1) **pace was km-only and implemented five times** (`paceAnalysis.ts` + private copies in `sessionFeedback`, `sessionReframe`, `aerobicTrend`, `strava.ts`); pace is a *rate*, so 5:30/km is 8:51/mi, not "5:30/mi" — `lib/format.ts → formatPace` is now the single owner (INV-FMT-001) and `paceAnalysis` re-exports it. `strava.ts` has a different signature and stays with FMT-02. (2) **few-shot examples quote km in inputs *and* outputs** ("Cut it 2km short"), so converting only the data makes the model echo km; mi prompts carry an explicit output-unit override and the exemplars stay in km (they teach voice, not units). **Precision deliberately not taken from the UI:** `formatDistance` rounds for calm (5.7→"6km"), but a model told "6km" for a 5.7km session narrates a shortfall the engine doesn't recognise (§66 fires from `planAdjustment.ts` arithmetic, not prose) — new `formatDistanceForPrompt` converts units and keeps each call site's precision. **Coaching Board not convened (ADR-017):** unit conversion is display formatting (ADR-015, outside the board's remit), §66 unaffected, precision unchanged — no coaching delta. **Fixed in passing:** `sessionFeedback` rendered `5:30/km/km avg` (double suffix); `LIMITER.PACE_FADE_REFERENCE_SEC` promoted from a prose literal (INV-CFG-003, value unchanged at 15); and the injected helper was renamed `fmtDist` after a plain `dist` silently shadowed local `dist` string vars in 3 builders — invisible to tsc because the helper was a valid identifier, which would have interpolated a stringified arrow function into the prompt. **FMT-03:** `SessionHero` deleted (166 lines, zero call sites), taking the last legacy `rts_metric_*` localStorage read with it. **Zero-regression is mechanical:** `units` defaults to `'km'` at every builder and `lib/format.units.test.ts` asserts an omitted-units prompt is byte-identical to an explicit-km one. **Residual:** reframe golden suite not re-run — manual AI-output comparison, and km prompts are byte-identical so cases A–D are unaffected; worth a pass before a miles user sees it. Verified: tsc clean + 480 tests (15 new). Commit `0574e6e`. |
| Daily push honours the user's distance unit (PUSH-UNITS-01) | FREE | 2026-08-15 *(backfilled — shipped earlier in the ADR-015 Phase-2 pass; backlog entry was never closed)* | The daily-session push renders distance in the user's own unit rather than a hardcoded `'km'`. `app/api/push/send-daily/route.ts` selects `preferred_units, preferred_metric`, normalises via `normalizeDisplayPrefs`, resolves the metric through the full ADR-015 ladder (per-session override → plan `primary_metric` → global preference) via `resolveSessionMetric`, and passes the resulting `PushDisplay` to `buildDailyPushTitle`; `lib/coaching/voiceLines.ts` renders through `formatSessionMetric` (INV-FMT-001 / INV-PREF-001). **Verified 2026-08-15 by code read + existing coverage** — `voiceLines.test.ts` asserts an 8.04672 km tempo renders as `"The hard one's today. 5mi."` for a mi reader and `"40 min"` when the resolved metric is duration. No new code was required; this entry closes a stale backlog item. Rounding (the 5.7-vs-6km class) was fixed in the same earlier pass. |
| Plan difficulty band (§44 amendment / §31) + §34 coverage-guard revival | FREE (band; co-shipped invariant hygiene is infra — nothing newly gated) | 2026-08-18 | *Not in backlog — emergent from a Coaching Board review this session.* **Board CORRECT WITH AMENDMENT + SLT FREE (unanimous).** Every generated plan now carries an ordinal demand label `meta.difficulty_band` ∈ {comfortable, demanding, very_demanding} + a `difficulty_note` on the demanding tiers, describing the plan's demand on the runner's chosen timeline. Derived in `generateRulePlan()` (`lib/plan/ruleEngine.ts`) **only** from prep-time margin + `compression_classification` — a *pre-generation feasibility* read, structurally kept distinct from the PAID numeric confidence score (a *quality* read) so the two can never contradict. The refusal tier stays the §44 `block` (throws before a plan exists). **Ordinal, never a percentage** — Board veto on false precision from one benchmark + one max HR; label describes demand-on-the-timeline, not a verdict on the runner. **Artifacts:** principle §44/§31; numeric `GENERATION_CONFIG.DIFFICULTY_COMFORTABLE_MARGIN_WEEKS = 2`; invariants `INV-PLAN-DIFFICULTY-ANNOTATED` (every plan carries a band) + `INV-PLAN-DIFFICULTY-NEVER-FRONTS-UNSAFE` (a `warned` plan reads very_demanding; a `constrained_by_inputs` plan is never comfortable); gate `FEATURE_GATES.FREE_ALWAYS += plan_difficulty_band`; `PlanMeta` types. **Co-shipped infra (no behavioural delta):** revived the §34 invariant-coverage guard (`scripts/r2-coverage-check.ts`), which had silently died on date-rotted fixtures (dates now relative + Sunday-anchored — can't rot, no blocked-day collision); registered 3 emitted-but-unregistered maintenance invariants (`INV-MAINT-CADENCE` / `-INJURY-EASY-ONLY` / `-REENGAGEMENT-WINDOW`); removed dead `INV-PLAN-THEME-MATCHES-PRESCRIPTION` (retired by GEN-FIX-06 / incident N4) and reconciled §27/§41/§57 + `plan-invariants.md` refs to the live stricter `INV-PLAN-COPY-MATCHES-SESSIONS`; deleted temp `__coachReviewGen.test.ts`. Verified: tsc clean + §34 guard green (54 invariants, 3 canonical cases) + property fuzzer 414,720 plans/0 violations + archetype 11/11 + lib/plan 123/123 + golden snapshots additive-only. Commit `3b7a03f`. |
| Difficulty-band follow-ups — tempo cue + enricher awareness + preview UI | FREE (band UI + cue; enricher awareness is PAID infra) | 2026-08-18 | Three follow-ons to the difficulty band above. **(A) Tempo controlled-effort cue (§1/Q2, FREE):** the FIRST genuine threshold session of a plan carries one execution cue ("Controlled effort — if you can't say a short sentence, you've drifted into the grey zone.", echoing `BRAND.voiceAnchor`). The salvageable half of the Coaching Board's Q2 ruling ("learn the cue, refuse the [Norwegian] method"); SLT chose first-threshold-only (taught once, not every session). Keyed on `catalogueRow.category === 'threshold'` (never a label — INV-CLASS); skipped for vo2max, goal-pace overrides and deload weeks. Plan-level `cueCtx` flag threaded `generateRulePlan → buildWeekSessions → makeQualitySession`, which owns the decision via its own predicates (no duplicated classification). Rule-engine copy, no AIMark. **(B) Enricher band-awareness (PAID infra):** `enrich.ts` passes `difficulty_band` + `difficulty_note` into the prompt with a consistency instruction, so AI voice (coach_intro/notes) can't contradict the band (no calling a very_demanding plan a breeze). No prescription change → no board. **(C) Preview UI (FREE):** `DifficultyCard` on the plan-generation `preview` step, above the PAID `ConfidenceBadge` (feasibility over quality — never competing scores). SLT-locked framing (2026-08-18): **silent on 'comfortable' and legacy plans** (no band); demanding tiers render one honest amber (`--warn`) sentence — the note, not a label/score; very_demanding also lists the §44 plan-level alternatives (reuses `meta.prep_time_alternatives`, never an upsell). Rule-engine output → **no AIMark**; amber coaching-caution, never `--danger`; tokens only. Verified: tsc clean + property fuzzer 414,720/0 (INV-PLAN-COACH-NOTES-MATCH-INTENT holds) + §34 guard + archetype 11/11 + lib/plan 123/123 + golden additive-only (cue once per plan) + render-decision check across all 4 band states. **UI type-checked + data-path-verified; not browser-driven (no agent-browser in the build session — visual paint unconfirmed, reuses the live `coach_intro` card pattern).** Commit `4dee658`. |
| Session catalogue single source of truth (SC-00) | FREE (infra) | 2026-08-20 | The generator never queried the `session_catalogue` Supabase table — it reads the in-repo mirror `lib/plan/sessionCatalogueData.ts`, which had drifted 2 rows + 1 eligibility change ahead of the DB for ~4 months. | Second quality session — placement fix + minimum-days rule (SC-01, CD-20) | FREE | 2026-08-20 | Two defects in `ruleEngine.ts` second-quality placement: `'fri'` was absent from the hardcoded candidate list `['tue','thu','mon']` (and for the common long-Sunday/quality-Wednesday shape, Friday is the ONLY day satisfying both 48h gaps), and the code took the first FREE day then tested spacing, so a failing candidate ENDED the search. Spacing now filters candidates first. Gated on `MIN_TRAINING_DAYS_FOR_SECOND_QUALITY = 5`, derived from volume arithmetic (quality consumes 32.4% of weekly volume; easy is capped at `long / 1.25` per §9, so a 4-day week reaches ~58% of the 67.6% needed). The hardcoded list had been blocking 4-day weeks by accident while wrongly denying 5-day runners. Withheld session recorded as `rule_adjustments` entry `V8-second-quality-min-days` (McMillan's amendment — a withheld session is a decision, not a silent absence). §8 amended + `INV-PLAN-SECOND-QUALITY-MIN-DAYS`. Commit `7af7b62`. |
| Label integrity — aerobic rows can't wear a quality name (SC-02) | FREE | 2026-08-20 | §19 breach closed in both halves: an aerobic catalogue row selected into a quality slot kept its aerobic pacing while presenting as quality work. Classification keys off `catalogueRow.category` (structural), never a display label — INV-CLASS / D-17, via the `sessionRole` single owner. Tests in `lib/plan/labelIntegrityAerobic.test.ts`.
